directions contribute to the excess error. In technical terms, let
\mathcal{P}
be the set
\left\{e_{i_{1}},\ldots,e_{i_{d/2}}\right\}
. Note that
|\mathcal{P}|=d/2
. Then,

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\mathcal{R}\left(\bm{x}_{t}\right)

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{2d}\sum_{i=1}^{d}\mathbb{E}\left(\left\langle{e},{e_{i}}\right\rangle-\frac{1}{\sqrt{d}}\right)^{2},

\displaystyle\geq\frac{1}{2d}\sum_{e\not\in\mathcal{P}}\frac{1}{d}=\frac{d-|\mathcal{P}|}{2d^{2}}=\frac{1}{4d}.

Lemma 17

For any initial point
\bm{x}_{0}
and Hessian
\mathbf{H}^{\prime}
with
\mathrm{Tr}{\left(\mathbf{H}^{\prime}\right)}=1
, there exists a distribution
\rho^{\prime}
which prevent acceleration.

Proof
Let

\mathbf{H}^{\prime}=\sum_{i=1}^{d}p_{i}e_{i}e_{i}^{\top}.

The excess risk on any noise less problem with
\bm{x}_{*}^{\prime}
as optimum and
\mathbf{H}^{\prime}
as Hessian can be written as,

\displaystyle\mathcal{R}\left(\bm{x}\right)

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{2}\left(\bm{x}-\bm{x}_{*}^{\prime}\right)^{\top}\mathbf{H}^{\prime}\left(\bm{x}-\bm{x}_{*}^{\prime}\right),

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{2}\sum_{i=1}^{d}p_{i}\left(\left\langle{\bm{x}-\bm{x}_{*}^{\prime}},{e_{i}}\right\rangle\right)^{2}=\frac{1}{2d}\sum_{i=1}^{d}\left(\left\langle{\bm{x}-\bm{x}_{0}},{e_{i}}\right\rangle+\left\langle{\bm{x}_{0}-\bm{x}_{*}^{\prime}},{e_{i}}\right\rangle\right)^{2},

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{2}\sum_{i=1}^{d}p_{i}\left(\left\langle{\bm{x}-\bm{x}_{0}},{e_{i}}\right\rangle+\left\langle{\bm{x}_{0}-\bm{x}_{*}^{\prime}},{e_{i}}\right\rangle\right)^{2}.

Let
\rho^{\prime}
be the one hot basis distribution where
e_{i}
is sampled with probability
p_{i}
. Consider any stochastic first order algorithm
\mathcal{S}
for
t
iterations. Lets say
e_{i_{1}},\cdots,e_{i_{t}}
be the inputs from the stream till time
t
. From Definition
4
, the estimator
\bm{x}_{t}
after
t
iterations

\displaystyle\bm{x}_{t}\in\bm{x}_{0}+\mathit{span}\left\{\nabla_{0}f(\bm{x}_{0}),\nabla_{1}f(\bm{x}_{1}),\cdots,\nabla_{k-1}f(\bm{x}_{k-1})\right\}.

Note that for noiseless regression the stochastic gradient at time
k
is
\nabla_{k}f(\bm{x}_{k})=e_{i_{k}}(\left\langle{e_{i_{k}}},{\bm{x}_{k}-\bm{x}_{*}}\right\rangle
. Using the fact that it is always along the direction of
e_{i_{k}}
.

\displaystyle e\overset{\scriptstyle\mathrm{def}}{=}\bm{x}_{t}-\bm{x}_{0}\in\mathit{span}\left\{e_{i_{1}},\cdots,e_{i_{t}}\right\}.

Plugging this in the above expression for excess risk, we get,

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\mathcal{R}\left(\bm{x}_{t}\right)

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{2}\sum_{i=1}^{d}p_{i}\mathbb{E}\left(\left\langle{e},{e_{i}}\right\rangle+\left\langle{\bm{x}_{0}-\bm{x}_{*}^{\prime}},{e_{i}}\right\rangle\right)^{2}.

If none of the
e_{i_{k}}
’s , for
k\leq t
are
e_{i}
then
\left\langle{e},{e_{i}}\right\rangle=0
. This event occurs with a probability
\left(1-p_{i}\right)^{t}
. Hence, with probability
\left(1-p_{i}\right)^{t}
,
\left\langle{e},{e_{i}}\right\rangle=0
. Taking this into consideration,

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left(\left\langle{e},{e_{i}}\right\rangle+\left\langle{\bm{x}_{0}-\bm{x}_{*}^{\prime}},{e_{i}}\right\rangle\right)^{2}\geq\left(1-p_{i}\right)^{t}\left\langle{\bm{x}_{0}-\bm{x}_{*}^{\prime}},{e_{i}}\right\rangle^{2}

Hence,

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\mathcal{R}\left(\bm{x}_{t}\right)

\displaystyle\geq\frac{1}{2}\sum_{i=1}^{d}p_{i}\left(1-p_{i}\right)^{t}\left\langle{\bm{x}_{0}-\bm{x}_{*}^{\prime}},{e_{i}}\right\rangle^{2}

Noting that the right hand side corresponds to the performance of gradient descent with step size
1
after
t/2
iterations. In conclusion, performance of gradient descent is better than any stochastic algorithm. Hence, direct acceleration with this oracle defined by
\rho^{\prime}
is not possible.

with space constrains. Note that the version with

-space decrease at rate

with

decays at rate

.

In Figure

, we demonstrate that the with additional space the speed of the decay can be improved. Note that the version with
O(d^{2})
-space decrease at rate
1/t^{2}
where Algorithm
4
with
O(d)
decays at rate
d/t^{2}
. The set up for this experiment is same as the setup of the plot on Last Iterate described in Section
7
. The
O(d)-curve
corresponds to the Algorithm
4
with SGD oracle Eq.(
6
) with step sizes
\alpha=\nicefrac{{1}}{{3~d\mathrm{Tr}{\mathbf{H}}}},\beta=\nicefrac{{1}}{{3\mathrm{Tr}{\mathbf{H}}}}
where
\mathbf{H}
is the covariance of Gaussian data. The
O(d^{2})-curve
corresponds to the Algorithm
4
with running average SGD oracle in Section
5
with step sizes
\alpha=\nicefrac{{1}}{{3\mathrm{Tr}{\mathbf{H}}}},\beta=\nicefrac{{1}}{{3\mathrm{Tr}{\mathbf{H}}}}
.

Appendix C
Bias and Variance

In this section, we investigate the recursions of expected covariance of the bias and variance process.

C.1
Our technique for Bias

The work of
Jain et al. 2018b
introduces a novel Lyapunov function
c_{1}{\mathbb{E}}\left[\bigl\|{\bm{y}_{t}-\bm{x}_{*}}\bigr\|^{2}\right]+c_{2}{\mathbb{E}}\left[\bigl\|{\bm{z}_{t}-\bm{x}_{*}}\bigr\|^{2}_{\mathbf{H}^{-1}}\right]
, for some constants
c_{1},c_{2}
for the analysis of bias error to show accelerated SGD rates for strongly convex least squares. Using similar Lyapunov function on the non-strongly convex version of Nesterov acceleration algorithm, in
Even et al. 2021
, a rate of convergence for
{\mathbb{E}}\left[\bigl\|{\bm{x}_{t}-\bm{x}_{*}}\bigr\|^{2}\right]
,i.e,

{\mathbb{E}}\left[\bigl\|{\bm{x}_{t}-\bm{x}_{*}}\bigr\|^{2}\right]\lesssim\frac{\bigl\|{\bm{x}_{0}-\bm{x}_{*}}\bigr\|^{2}_{\mathbf{H}^{-1}}}{t^{2}}.

is shown. Even with this result, it is still unclear how to relate excess error
{\mathbb{E}}\left[\bigl\|{\bm{x}_{t}-\bm{x}_{*}}\bigr\|^{2}_{\mathbf{H}}\right]
and distance of initialization
\bigl\|{\bm{x}_{0}-\bm{x}_{*}}\bigr\|^{2}
. Note that for non-strongly convex functions
\bigl\|{\bm{x}_{0}-\bm{x}_{*}}\bigr\|^{2}_{\mathbf{H}^{-1}}
can be arbitrarily large in comparison to
\bigl\|{\bm{x}_{0}-\bm{x}_{*}}\bigr\|^{2}
. As there is an absence of direct Lyapunov techniques for bias error, it is needed to introduce a new method.

In recent times, many works
[
Zou et al. 2021
,
Varre et al. 2021
]
have studied the sharp characterization of bias process in SGD to understand the performance of SGD for over-parameterized least squares. In
Zou et al. 2021
, it is shown that sum of covariance i.e.
\sum_{i=0}^{t}{\mathbb{E}}\left[\bm{\theta}_{b,i}\otimes\bm{\theta}_{b,i}\right]
of SGD at the limit
t\to\infty
is used to give sharp bounds for bias excess risk. Even this approach cannot be used to in our case for two reasons (a) the limit in the case of our accelerated methods still depends on
\bigl\|{\bm{x}_{0}-\bm{x}_{*}}\bigr\|^{2}_{\mathbf{H}^{-1}}
which can be arbitrarily large (b) this requires more restricting uniformly bounded kurtosis assumption. In our approach we give sharp estimates for finite sum of covariance and relate them to the sum of covariance for the Algorithm
4
with exact gradients (see Lemma
19
). This method gives us the bounds on the excess risk of bias part. Also, note that our approach does not require the assumption of bounded uniform kurtosis and works with standard fourth moment Assumption
1
.

C.2
Bias

Recalling the recursion Eq.(
12
), we have

\displaystyle\bm{\theta}_{b,t+1}=\mathcal{J}_{t}~\bm{\theta}_{b,t}.

(36)

For all
t\geq 0
, we use the following notation for the sake of brevity

\mathcal{C}_{t}\overset{\scriptstyle\mathrm{def}}{=}\mathbb{E}\left[\bm{\theta}_{b,t}\otimes\bm{\theta}_{b,t}\right].

Lemma 18

For
t\geq 0
, the covariance of the bias iterates is determined by the recursion,

\displaystyle\mathcal{C}_{t+1}

\displaystyle=\widetilde{\mathcal{T}}^{~\left(t+1\right)}\circ\mathcal{C}_{0}+\sum_{k=0}^{t}\widetilde{\mathcal{T}}^{k}\circ{\mathcal{M}}\circ\mathcal{C}_{t-k}

Proof

From the recursion on the bias covariance of Lemma
14
, we have

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}\left[\bm{\theta}_{b,t+1}\otimes\bm{\theta}_{b,t+1}\right]

\displaystyle=\mathcal{T}\circ{\mathbb{E}}\left[\bm{\theta}_{b,t}\otimes\bm{\theta}_{b,t}\right],

\displaystyle\mathcal{C}_{t+1}

\displaystyle=\mathcal{T}\circ\mathcal{C}_{t}=\left(\widetilde{\mathcal{T}}+{\mathcal{M}}\right)\circ\mathcal{C}_{t},\quad\text{from Lemma~\ref{lem:operator}},

\displaystyle=\widetilde{\mathcal{T}}\circ\mathcal{C}_{t}+{\mathcal{M}}\circ\mathcal{C}_{t},

\displaystyle=\widetilde{\mathcal{T}}\circ\left(\widetilde{\mathcal{T}}\circ\mathcal{C}_{t-1}+{\mathcal{M}}\circ\mathcal{C}_{t-1}\right)+{\mathcal{M}}\circ\mathcal{C}_{t}=\widetilde{\mathcal{T}}^{2}\circ\mathcal{C}_{t-1}+\widetilde{\mathcal{T}}\circ{\mathcal{M}}\circ\mathcal{C}_{t-1}+{\mathcal{M}}\circ\mathcal{C}_{t}.

Expanding this recursively we get the following expression

\displaystyle\mathcal{C}_{t+1}

\displaystyle=\widetilde{\mathcal{T}}^{~\left(t+1\right)}\circ\mathcal{C}_{0}+\sum_{k=0}^{t}\widetilde{\mathcal{T}}^{k}\circ{\mathcal{M}}\circ\mathcal{C}_{t-k}.

Lemma 19

For
T\geq 0
,

\displaystyle\left(\mathcal{I}-\left(\mathcal{I}-\widetilde{\mathcal{T}}\right)^{-1}\circ{\mathcal{M}}\right)\circ\sum_{t=0}^{T}\mathcal{C}_{t}

\displaystyle\preccurlyeq\left(\sum_{t=0}^{T}\widetilde{\mathcal{T}}^{~\left(t\right)}\right)\circ\mathcal{C}_{0}.

(37)

Proof

From Lemma
18
,

\displaystyle\mathcal{C}_{t}

\displaystyle=\widetilde{\mathcal{T}}^{~\left(t\right)}\circ\mathcal{C}_{0}+\sum_{k=0}^{t-1}\widetilde{\mathcal{T}}^{t-k-1}\circ{\mathcal{M}}\circ\mathcal{C}_{k}.

Consider the following summation,

\displaystyle\sum_{t=0}^{T}\mathcal{C}_{t}

\displaystyle=\sum_{t}\left[\widetilde{\mathcal{T}}^{~\left(t\right)}\circ\mathcal{C}_{0}+\sum_{k=0}^{t-1}\widetilde{\mathcal{T}}^{t-1-k}\circ{\mathcal{M}}\circ\mathcal{C}_{k}\right],

\displaystyle=\left(\sum_{t=0}^{T}\widetilde{\mathcal{T}}^{~\left(t\right)}\right)\circ\mathcal{C}_{0}+\sum_{t=0}^{T}\sum_{k=0}^{t-1}\widetilde{\mathcal{T}}^{t-1-k}\circ{\mathcal{M}}\circ\mathcal{C}_{k}.

Exchanging the summations for the second part,

\displaystyle\sum_{t=0}^{T}\mathcal{C}_{t}

\displaystyle=\left(\sum_{t=0}^{T}\widetilde{\mathcal{T}}^{~\left(t\right)}\right)\circ\mathcal{C}_{0}+\sum_{k=0}^{T-1}\sum_{t=k+1}^{T}\widetilde{\mathcal{T}}^{t-1-k}\circ{\mathcal{M}}\circ\mathcal{C}_{k},

\displaystyle=\left(\sum_{t=0}^{T}\widetilde{\mathcal{T}}^{~\left(t\right)}\right)\circ\mathcal{C}_{0}+\sum_{k=0}^{T-1}\left[\sum_{t=0}^{T-k-1}\widetilde{\mathcal{T}}^{t}\right]\circ{\mathcal{M}}\circ\mathcal{C}_{k}.

Note that
\mathcal{C}_{k}
is PSD, for
k\geq 0
and
{\mathcal{M}}
is positive. Hence,
{\mathcal{M}}\circ\mathcal{C}_{k}
is PSD. Since
\widetilde{\mathcal{T}}\succcurlyeq 0
(to be precise
\widetilde{\mathcal{T}}\circ{\mathcal{M}}\circ\mathcal{C}_{k}\succcurlyeq 0
, but we drop this for simplicity of writing), we can say the following things,

\displaystyle\forall t\geq 0,~~\widetilde{\mathcal{T}}^{~t}

\displaystyle\succcurlyeq 0,

\displaystyle\textrm{for any }t{{}^{\prime}}\geq 0,~~\sum_{t>t{{}^{\prime}}}\widetilde{\mathcal{T}}^{~t}

\displaystyle\succcurlyeq 0,

\displaystyle\textrm{Hence, for any }t{{}^{\prime}}\geq 0,~~\sum_{t\geq 0}\widetilde{\mathcal{T}}^{~t}

\displaystyle\succcurlyeq\sum_{t=0}^{t{{}^{\prime}}}\widetilde{\mathcal{T}}^{~t}.

\displaystyle\sum_{t=0}^{T}\mathcal{C}_{t}

\displaystyle\preccurlyeq\left(\sum_{t=0}^{T}\widetilde{\mathcal{T}}^{~\left(t\right)}\right)\circ\mathcal{C}_{0}+\sum_{k=0}^{T-1}\left[\sum_{t\geq 0}\widetilde{\mathcal{T}}^{~t}\right]\circ{\mathcal{M}}\circ\mathcal{C}_{k}.

Here we use the fact that

\sum_{t\geq 0}\widetilde{\mathcal{T}}^{~t}=\left(\mathcal{I}-\widetilde{\mathcal{T}}\right)^{-1}.\

\displaystyle\sum_{t=0}^{T}\mathcal{C}_{t}

\displaystyle\preccurlyeq\left(\sum_{t=0}^{T}\widetilde{\mathcal{T}}^{~\left(t\right)}\right)\circ\mathcal{C}_{0}+\sum_{k=0}^{T-1}\left(\mathcal{I}-\widetilde{\mathcal{T}}\right)^{-1}\circ{\mathcal{M}}\circ\mathcal{C}_{k},

\displaystyle\preccurlyeq\left(\sum_{t=0}^{T}\widetilde{\mathcal{T}}^{~\left(t\right)}\right)\circ\mathcal{C}_{0}+\left(\mathcal{I}-\widetilde{\mathcal{T}}\right)^{-1}\circ{\mathcal{M}}\circ\sum_{k=0}^{T-1}\mathcal{C}_{k}.

Using the fact that
\mathcal{C}_{T}\succcurlyeq 0
, we have

\sum_{k=0}^{T-1}\mathcal{C}_{k}\leq\sum_{k=0}^{T}\mathcal{C}_{k}

Hence,

\displaystyle\sum_{t=0}^{T}\mathcal{C}_{t}

\displaystyle\preccurlyeq\left(\sum_{t=0}^{T}\widetilde{\mathcal{T}}^{~\left(t\right)}\right)\circ\mathcal{C}_{0}+\left(\mathcal{I}-\widetilde{\mathcal{T}}\right)^{-1}\circ{\mathcal{M}}\circ\sum_{k=0}^{T}\mathcal{C}_{k}.

From this we can prove the lemma

\displaystyle\left(\mathcal{I}-\left(\mathcal{I}-\widetilde{\mathcal{T}}\right)^{-1}\circ{\mathcal{M}}\right)\circ\sum_{t=0}^{T}\mathcal{C}_{t}

\displaystyle\preccurlyeq\left(\sum_{t=0}^{T}\widetilde{\mathcal{T}}^{~\left(t\right)}\right)\circ\mathcal{C}_{0}.

Lemma 20

With the stepsizes satisfying
(\alpha+2\beta)R^{2}\leq 1,\alpha\leq\frac{\beta}{2\tilde{\kappa}}
the sum of covariance can be bounded by

\displaystyle\sum_{t=0}^{T}\left\langle{\begin{bmatrix}\mathbf{H}&\mathbf{H}\\
\mathbf{H}&\mathbf{H}\end{bmatrix}},{{\mathbb{E}}\left[\bm{\theta}_{b,t}\otimes\bm{\theta}_{b,t}\right]}\right\rangle\leq\min\left\{\frac{3(T+1)}{\alpha},\frac{12(T+1)(T+2)}{\beta}\right\}\bigl\|{\bm{x}_{0}-\bm{x}_{*}}\bigr\|^{2}.

Proof

From Lemma
19
,

\displaystyle\left(\mathcal{I}-\left(\mathcal{I}-\widetilde{\mathcal{T}}\right)^{-1}\circ{\mathcal{M}}\right)\circ\sum_{t=0}^{T}\mathcal{C}_{t}

\displaystyle\preccurlyeq\left(\sum_{t=0}^{T}\widetilde{\mathcal{T}}^{~\left(t\right)}\right)\circ\mathcal{C}_{0},

\displaystyle\left\langle{\begin{bmatrix}\mathbf{H}&\mathbf{H}\\
\mathbf{H}&\mathbf{H}\end{bmatrix}},{\left(\mathcal{I}-\left(\mathcal{I}-\widetilde{\mathcal{T}}\right)^{-1}\circ{\mathcal{M}}\right)\circ\sum_{t=0}^{T}\mathcal{C}_{t}}\right\rangle

\displaystyle\leq\left\langle{\begin{bmatrix}\mathbf{H}&\mathbf{H}\\
\mathbf{H}&\mathbf{H}\end{bmatrix}},{\left(\sum_{t=0}^{T}\widetilde{\mathcal{T}}^{~\left(t\right)}\right)\circ\mathcal{C}_{0}}\right\rangle.

Using the definition of transpose of operators in Remark
11
, we get

\displaystyle\left\langle{\begin{bmatrix}\mathbf{H}&\mathbf{H}\\
\mathbf{H}&\mathbf{H}\end{bmatrix}},{\left(\mathcal{I}-\left(\mathcal{I}-\widetilde{\mathcal{T}}\right)^{-1}\circ{\mathcal{M}}\right)\circ\sum_{t=0}^{T}\mathcal{C}_{t}}\right\rangle=\left\langle{\left(\mathcal{I}-{\mathcal{M}}^{\top}\circ\left(\mathcal{I}-\widetilde{\mathcal{T}}^{\top}\right)^{-1}\right)\circ\Upsilon},{\sum_{t=0}^{T}\mathcal{C}_{t}}\right\rangle

(38)

As the condition on the stepsize is satisfied, we can use the fact that
\Upsilon
is almost eigen vector of
{\mathcal{M}}^{\top}\circ\left(\mathcal{I}-\widetilde{\mathcal{T}}^{\top}\right)^{-1}
from Lemmas
15
,
34
,

\displaystyle{\mathcal{M}}^{\top}\circ\left(\mathcal{I}-\widetilde{\mathcal{T}}^{\top}\right)^{-1}\circ\begin{bmatrix}\mathbf{H}&\mathbf{H}\\
\mathbf{H}&\mathbf{H}\end{bmatrix}

\displaystyle\preccurlyeq\frac{2}{3}\begin{bmatrix}\mathbf{H}&\mathbf{H}\\
\mathbf{H}&\mathbf{H}\end{bmatrix}

\displaystyle\left(\mathcal{I}-{\mathcal{M}}^{\top}\circ\left(\mathcal{I}-\widetilde{\mathcal{T}}^{\top}\right)^{-1}\right)\circ\begin{bmatrix}\mathbf{H}&\mathbf{H}\\
\mathbf{H}&\mathbf{H}\end{bmatrix}

\displaystyle\succcurlyeq\frac{1}{3}\begin{bmatrix}\mathbf{H}&\mathbf{H}\\
\mathbf{H}&\mathbf{H}\end{bmatrix}.

Combining them we get,

\displaystyle\frac{1}{3}\left\langle{\begin{bmatrix}\mathbf{H}&\mathbf{H}\\
\mathbf{H}&\mathbf{H}\end{bmatrix}},{\sum_{t=0}^{T}\mathcal{C}_{t}}\right\rangle

\displaystyle\leq\left\langle{\begin{bmatrix}\mathbf{H}&\mathbf{H}\\
\mathbf{H}&\mathbf{H}\end{bmatrix}},{\left(\sum_{t=0}^{T}\widetilde{\mathcal{T}}^{~\left(t\right)}\right)\circ\mathcal{C}_{0}}\right\rangle,

\displaystyle\sum_{t=0}^{T}\left\langle{\begin{bmatrix}\mathbf{H}&\mathbf{H}\\
\mathbf{H}&\mathbf{H}\end{bmatrix}},{\mathcal{C}_{t}}\right\rangle

\displaystyle\leq 3\sum_{t=0}^{T}\left\langle{\begin{bmatrix}\mathbf{H}&\mathbf{H}\\
\mathbf{H}&\mathbf{H}\end{bmatrix}},{\left(\widetilde{\mathcal{T}}^{~\left(t\right)}\right)\circ\mathcal{C}_{0}}\right\rangle.

Note that
\bm{\theta}_{b,0}=\bm{\theta}_{0}
gives
\mathcal{C}_{0}=\bm{\theta}_{0}\otimes\bm{\theta}_{0}
. From Lemma
27
, for
0\leqslant t\leqslant T
,

\displaystyle\left\langle{\begin{bmatrix}\mathbf{H}&\mathbf{H}\\
\mathbf{H}&\mathbf{H}\end{bmatrix}},{\widetilde{\mathcal{T}}^{~\left(t\right)}\circ\ \left[\bm{\theta}_{0}\otimes\bm{\theta}_{0}\right]}\right\rangle\leq\min\left\{\frac{1}{\alpha},\frac{8(t+1)}{\beta}\right\}\bigl\|{\bm{x}_{0}-\bm{x}_{*}}\bigr\|^{2}.

Summing this for
0\leq t\leq T
proves the lemma.

C.3
Bias Last Iterate

Lemma 21
(Final Iterate Risk)

Under Assumptions
4
and the step-sizes satisfying
\alpha\leq\beta\leq 1/L
. For
T\geq 0
, the last iterate excess error can be determined by the following discrete Volterra integral

\displaystyle\mathsf{f}\left({\bm{\theta}_{b,T}}\right)

\displaystyle\leqslant\min\left\{\frac{1}{\alpha},\frac{8(T+1)}{\beta}\right\}\bigl\|{\bm{x}_{0}-\bm{x}_{*}}\bigr\|^{2}+\sum_{t=0}^{T-1}\mathsf{g}\left({\mathbf{H},t-k-1}\right)\mathsf{f}\left({\bm{\theta}_{b,t}}\right),

where
\mathsf{f}\left({\bm{\theta}_{b,t}}\right)\overset{\scriptstyle\mathrm{def}}{=}\left\langle{\begin{bmatrix}\mathbf{H}&\mathbf{H}\\
\mathbf{H}&\mathbf{H}\end{bmatrix}},{{\mathbb{E}}\left[\bm{\theta}_{b,t}\otimes\bm{\theta}_{b,t}\right]}\right\rangle
and the kernel

\displaystyle\mathsf{g}\left({\mathbf{H},t}\right)\overset{\scriptstyle\mathrm{def}}{=}\kappa\left\langle{\Upsilon},{\widetilde{\mathcal{T}}^{t}\circ\bm{\Xi}}\right\rangle.

(39)

Proof

Invoking Lemma
18
and
\bm{\theta}_{b,0}=\bm{\theta}_{0}
gives

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}\left[\bm{\theta}_{b,t}\otimes\bm{\theta}_{b,t}\right]

\displaystyle=\widetilde{\mathcal{T}}^{~\left(t\right)}\circ\left[\bm{\theta}_{0}\otimes\bm{\theta}_{0}\right]+\sum_{k=0}^{t-1}\widetilde{\mathcal{T}}^{t-k-1}\circ{\mathcal{M}}\circ{\mathbb{E}}\left[\bm{\theta}_{b,k}\otimes\bm{\theta}_{b,k}\right].

Using Lemma
32
, note
\Upsilon
is defined at Eq.(
31
)

\displaystyle{\mathcal{M}}\circ{\mathbb{E}}\left[\bm{\theta}_{b,k}\otimes\bm{\theta}_{b,k}\right]

\displaystyle\preccurlyeq\kappa\left\langle{\Upsilon},{{\mathbb{E}}\left[\bm{\theta}_{b,k}\otimes\bm{\theta}_{b,k}\right]}\right\rangle\bm{\Xi}.

Using this and fact that
\mathcal{T}
is positive and
\bm{\Xi}
is PSD,

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}\left[\bm{\theta}_{b,t}\otimes\bm{\theta}_{b,t}\right]

\displaystyle\preccurlyeq\widetilde{\mathcal{T}}^{~\left(t\right)}\circ\left[\bm{\theta}_{0}\otimes\bm{\theta}_{0}\right]+\sum_{k=0}^{t-1}\widetilde{\mathcal{T}}^{t-k-1}\circ\kappa\left\langle{\Upsilon},{{\mathbb{E}}\left[\bm{\theta}_{b,k}\otimes\bm{\theta}_{b,k}\right]}\right\rangle\bm{\Xi}.

Taking the scalar product with
\Upsilon
on both sides, gives us

\displaystyle\left\langle{\Upsilon},{{\mathbb{E}}\left[\bm{\theta}_{b,t}\otimes\bm{\theta}_{b,t}\right]}\right\rangle\leq\left\langle{\Upsilon},{\widetilde{\mathcal{T}}^{~\left(t\right)}\circ\left[\bm{\theta}_{0}\otimes\bm{\theta}_{0}\right]}\right\rangle+\sum_{k=0}^{t-1}\left\langle{\Upsilon},{\widetilde{\mathcal{T}}^{t-k-1}\circ\bm{\Xi}}\right\rangle\kappa\left\langle{\Upsilon},{{\mathbb{E}}\left[\bm{\theta}_{b,k}\otimes\bm{\theta}_{b,k}\right]}\right\rangle.

From Lemma
27
, we get,

\displaystyle\left\langle{\Upsilon},{{\mathbb{E}}\left[\bm{\theta}_{b,t}\otimes\bm{\theta}_{b,t}\right]}\right\rangle\leq\min\left\{\frac{1}{\alpha},\frac{8(t+1)}{\beta}\right\}\bigl\|{\bm{x}_{0}-\bm{x}_{*}}\bigr\|^{2}+\sum_{k=0}^{t-1}\left\langle{\Upsilon},{\widetilde{\mathcal{T}}^{t-k-1}\circ\bm{\Xi}}\right\rangle\kappa\left\langle{\Upsilon},{{\mathbb{E}}\left[\bm{\theta}_{b,k}\otimes\bm{\theta}_{b,k}\right]}\right\rangle.

The definition of
\mathsf{f}\left({\bm{\theta}_{b,t}}\right)
proves the lemma.

Lemma 22

With

(\alpha+2\beta)\leq\frac{1}{\kappa\mathrm{Tr}{\mathbf{H}}},\alpha\leq\frac{\beta}{2\kappa d}
,
after
t
iterations of Algorithm
4
the bias excess error,

\displaystyle\left\langle{\begin{bmatrix}\mathbf{H}&\mathbf{H}\\
\mathbf{H}&\mathbf{H}\end{bmatrix}},{\bm{\theta}_{b,t}\otimes\bm{\theta}_{b,t}}\right\rangle

\displaystyle\leqslant\min\left\{\frac{3}{\alpha},\frac{24(t+1)}{\beta}\right\}\bigl\|{\bm{x}_{0}-\bm{x}_{*}}\bigr\|^{2}.

Proof

From Lemma
21
,

\displaystyle\left\langle{\Upsilon},{{\mathbb{E}}\left[\bm{\theta}_{b,t}\otimes\bm{\theta}_{b,t}\right]}\right\rangle\leq\min\left\{\frac{1}{\alpha},\frac{8(t+1)}{\beta}\right\}\bigl\|{\bm{x}_{0}-\bm{x}_{*}}\bigr\|^{2}+\sum_{k=0}^{t-1}\left\langle{\Upsilon},{\widetilde{\mathcal{T}}^{t-k-1}\circ\bm{\Xi}}\right\rangle\kappa\left\langle{\Upsilon},{{\mathbb{E}}\left[\bm{\theta}_{b,k}\otimes\bm{\theta}_{b,k}\right]}\right\rangle.

Now we will use induction to show that
\left\langle{\Upsilon},{{\mathbb{E}}\left[\bm{\theta}_{b,k}\otimes\bm{\theta}_{b,k}\right]}\right\rangle
is bounded.

Induction Hypothesis

There exists a constant
C
, for all
0\leqslant k\leqslant t-1
,
\left\langle{\Upsilon},{{\mathbb{E}}\left[\bm{\theta}_{b,k}\otimes\bm{\theta}_{b,k}\right]}\right\rangle\leq C
. Using this,

\displaystyle\left\langle{\Upsilon},{{\mathbb{E}}\left[\bm{\theta}_{b,t}\otimes\bm{\theta}_{b,t}\right]}\right\rangle

\displaystyle\leq\min\left\{\frac{1}{\alpha},\frac{8(t+1)}{\beta}\right\}\bigl\|{\bm{x}_{0}-\bm{x}_{*}}\bigr\|^{2}+\sum_{k=0}^{t-1}\left\langle{\Upsilon},{\widetilde{\mathcal{T}}^{t-k-1}\circ\bm{\Xi}}\right\rangle\kappa C,

\displaystyle=\min\left\{\frac{1}{\alpha},\frac{8(t+1)}{\beta}\right\}\bigl\|{\bm{x}_{0}-\bm{x}_{*}}\bigr\|^{2}+\left\langle{\Upsilon},{\sum_{k=0}^{t-1}\widetilde{\mathcal{T}}^{t-k-1}\circ\bm{\Xi}}\right\rangle\kappa C.

As
\mathcal{T}
is positive and
\Upsilon,\bm{\Xi}
is PSD,

\displaystyle\left(\sum_{k=0}^{t-1}\widetilde{\mathcal{T}}^{k}\right)\circ\bm{\Xi}

\displaystyle\preccurlyeq\left(\sum_{k=0}^{\infty}\widetilde{\mathcal{T}}^{k}\right)\circ\bm{\Xi}=\left(1-\widetilde{\mathcal{T}}\right)^{\scriptscriptstyle-1}\circ\bm{\Xi}

Using this upperbound,

\displaystyle\left\langle{\Upsilon},{{\mathbb{E}}\left[\bm{\theta}_{b,t}\otimes\bm{\theta}_{b,t}\right]}\right\rangle

\displaystyle\leqslant\min\left\{\frac{1}{\alpha},\frac{8(t+1)}{\beta}\right\}\bigl\|{\bm{x}_{0}-\bm{x}_{*}}\bigr\|^{2}+\kappa C\left\langle{\Upsilon},{\left(1-\widetilde{\mathcal{T}}\right)^{\scriptscriptstyle-1}\circ\bm{\Xi}}\right\rangle.

(40)

As the step sizes are chosen accordingly, using Lemma
29
,

\displaystyle\kappa C\left\langle{\Upsilon},{\left(1-\widetilde{\mathcal{T}}\right)^{\scriptscriptstyle-1}\circ\bm{\Xi}}\right\rangle\leq\frac{2C}{3}.

Substituting these back in Eq.(
40
),

\displaystyle\left\langle{\Upsilon},{{\mathbb{E}}\left[\bm{\theta}_{b,t}\otimes\bm{\theta}_{b,t}\right]}\right\rangle

\displaystyle\leqslant\min\left\{\frac{1}{\alpha},\frac{8(t+1)}{\beta}\right\}\bigl\|{\bm{x}_{0}-\bm{x}_{*}}\bigr\|^{2}+\frac{2C}{3}.

If we choose
C
such that,

\displaystyle C

\displaystyle=\min\left\{\frac{3}{\alpha},\frac{24(t+1)}{\beta}\right\}\bigl\|{\bm{x}_{0}-\bm{x}_{*}}\bigr\|^{2},

\displaystyle\frac{2C}{3}

\displaystyle=\min\left\{\frac{2}{\alpha},\frac{16(t+1)}{\beta}\right\}\bigl\|{\bm{x}_{0}-\bm{x}_{*}}\bigr\|^{2},

\displaystyle\left\langle{\Upsilon},{{\mathbb{E}}\left[\bm{\theta}_{b,t}\otimes\bm{\theta}_{b,t}\right]}\right\rangle

\displaystyle\leqslant\min\left\{\frac{3}{\alpha},\frac{24(t+1)}{\beta}\right\}\bigl\|{\bm{x}_{0}-\bm{x}_{*}}\bigr\|^{2}=C.

Hence we have shown that
\left\langle{\Upsilon},{{\mathbb{E}}\left[\bm{\theta}_{b,t}\otimes\bm{\theta}_{b,t}\right]}\right\rangle\leq C
. From induction we can say that for all
T>0
,

\displaystyle\left\langle{\Upsilon},{{\mathbb{E}}\left[\bm{\theta}_{b,t}\otimes\bm{\theta}_{b,t}\right]}\right\rangle

\displaystyle\leqslant C\quad\text{where}\quad C=\min\left\{\frac{3}{\alpha},\frac{24(t+1)}{\beta}\right\}\bigl\|{\bm{x}_{0}-\bm{x}_{*}}\bigr\|^{2}\bigl\|{\bm{x}_{0}-\bm{x}_{*}}\bigr\|^{2}.

C.4
Variance

We start by extending the the definition of the random matrix
\mathcal{J}_{t}
,

Definition 23

For every
0\leq i\leq j
, define the random linear operator
\mathcal{J}\left(i,j\right)
as follows

\displaystyle\mathcal{J}(j,i)=\prod_{k=i}^{j-1}\mathcal{J}_{k}\quad and\quad\mathcal{J}(i,i)=\mathcal{I}.

(41)

Recalling the variance subproblem Eq.(
13
) and using the above definition,

\displaystyle\bm{\theta}_{v,0}=\begin{bmatrix}\bm{0}\\
\bm{0}\end{bmatrix},\qquad\bm{\theta}_{v,t}

\displaystyle=\mathcal{J}(t,t-1)\bm{\theta}_{v,t-1}+\bm{\epsilon_{t}}.

(42)

Using this recursion for
t-1
and expanding it, we will get the following

\displaystyle\bm{\theta}_{v,t}

\displaystyle=\mathcal{J}(t,t-1)\left(\mathcal{J}(t-1,t-2)\bm{\theta}_{v,t-2}+\bm{\epsilon_{t-1}}\right)+\bm{\epsilon_{t}}.

Using the definition of
\mathcal{J}(i,j)
Def.
23
,

\displaystyle\bm{\theta}_{v,t}

\displaystyle=\mathcal{J}(t,t-2)\bm{\theta}_{v,t-2}+\mathcal{J}(t,t-1)\bm{\epsilon_{t-1}}+\bm{\epsilon_{t}}.

Expanding it further for any
0\leq i\leq t
, we have the following expression

\displaystyle\bm{\theta}_{v,t}

\displaystyle=\mathcal{J}(t,i)\bm{\theta}_{v,i}+\sum_{k=i+1}^{t}\mathcal{J}(t,k)\bm{\epsilon_{k}}.

(43)

Lemma 24

With the recursion defined by Eq.(
42
) and the expected covariance of the
{\overline{\bm{\theta}}_{v,t}}

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[{\overline{\bm{\theta}}_{v,T}}\otimes{\overline{\bm{\theta}}_{v,T}}\right]

\displaystyle=\sum_{i=1}^{T}\left(\sum_{j\geq i}^{T}\mathcal{A}^{j-i}\right)\left\{{\mathcal{M}}\circ{\mathbb{E}}\left[\bm{\theta}_{v,i-1}\otimes\bm{\theta}_{v,i-1}\right]+{\mathbb{E}}\left[\bm{\epsilon_{i}}\otimes\bm{\epsilon_{i}}\right]\right\}\left(\sum_{j\geq i}^{T}\mathcal{A}^{j-i}\right)^{\top}.

Proof
Recall that

\displaystyle{\overline{\bm{\theta}}_{v,T}}=\sum_{t=0}^{T}\bm{\theta}_{v,t}.

Considering the covariance of
{\overline{\bm{\theta}}_{v,T}}
,

\displaystyle{\overline{\bm{\theta}}_{v,T}}\otimes{\overline{\bm{\theta}}_{v,T}}

\displaystyle=\left(\sum_{i=0}^{T}\bm{\theta}_{v,i}\right)\otimes\left(\sum_{j=0}^{T}\bm{\theta}_{v,j}\right),

\displaystyle=\sum_{i}\left(\bm{\theta}_{v,i}\otimes\bm{\theta}_{v,i}+\sum_{j>i}\left(\bm{\theta}_{v,j}\otimes\bm{\theta}_{v,i}+\bm{\theta}_{v,i}\otimes\bm{\theta}_{v,j}\right)\right).

Taking expectation and using the linearity of expectation we get,

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[{\overline{\bm{\theta}}_{v,T}}\otimes{\overline{\bm{\theta}}_{v,T}}\right]

\displaystyle=\sum_{i=1}^{T}\left[\mathbb{E}\left[\bm{\theta}_{v,i}\otimes\bm{\theta}_{v,i}\right]+\sum_{j>i}^{T}\left({\mathbb{E}}\left[\bm{\theta}_{v,j}\otimes\bm{\theta}_{v,i}\right]+{\mathbb{E}}\left[\bm{\theta}_{v,i}\otimes\bm{\theta}_{v,j}\right]\right)\right].

Note that from Eq.(
43
), we can write
\bm{\theta}_{v,j}\otimes\bm{\theta}_{v,i}
for
j>i
as follows

\displaystyle\bm{\theta}_{v,j}\otimes\bm{\theta}_{v,i}=\mathcal{J}(t,i)\bm{\theta}_{v,i}\otimes\bm{\theta}_{v,i}+\sum_{k=i+1}^{j}\mathcal{J}(t,k)\bm{\epsilon_{k}}\otimes\bm{\theta}_{v,i}.

Now taking the expectation,

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}\left[\bm{\theta}_{v,j}\otimes\bm{\theta}_{v,i}\right]={\mathbb{E}}\left[\mathcal{J}(t,i)\bm{\theta}_{v,i}\otimes\bm{\theta}_{v,i}\right]+\sum_{k=i+1}^{j}{\mathbb{E}}\left[\mathcal{J}(t,k)\bm{\epsilon_{k}}\otimes\bm{\theta}_{v,i}\right].

For all
k>i
,
\mathcal{J}(t,k),\bm{\epsilon_{k}}
is independent of
\bm{\theta}_{v,i}
,
\mathcal{J}(t,k),\bm{\epsilon_{k}}
are also independent from their definition. We have
{\mathbb{E}}\left[\mathcal{J}(t,k)\right]=\mathcal{A}^{t-k}
, and
{\mathbb{E}}\left[\bm{\epsilon_{k}}\right]=0
. Using these,

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}\left[\bm{\theta}_{v,j}\otimes\bm{\theta}_{v,i}\right]=\mathcal{A}^{j-i}{\mathbb{E}}\left[\bm{\theta}_{v,i}\otimes\bm{\theta}_{v,i}\right].

With the same reasoning, for
j\geq i
,

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}\left[\bm{\theta}_{v,i}\otimes\bm{\theta}_{v,j}\right]={\mathbb{E}}\left[\bm{\theta}_{v,i}\otimes\bm{\theta}_{v,i}\right]\left(\mathcal{A}^{\top}\right)^{j-i}.

Substituting the above here gives,

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[{\overline{\bm{\theta}}_{v,T}}\otimes{\overline{\bm{\theta}}_{v,T}}\right]

\displaystyle=\sum_{i=1}^{T}\left\{\mathbb{E}\left[\bm{\theta}_{v,i}\otimes\bm{\theta}_{v,i}\right]+\sum_{j>i}^{T}\left({\mathbb{E}}\left[\bm{\theta}_{v,j}\otimes\bm{\theta}_{v,i}\right]+{\mathbb{E}}\left[\bm{\theta}_{v,i}\otimes\bm{\theta}_{v,j}\right]\right)\right\},

\displaystyle=\sum_{i=1}^{T}\left\{\mathbb{E}\left[\bm{\theta}_{v,i}\otimes\bm{\theta}_{v,i}\right]+\left(\sum_{j>i}^{T}\mathcal{A}^{j-i}\right){\mathbb{E}}\left[\bm{\theta}_{v,i}\otimes\bm{\theta}_{v,i}\right]+{\mathbb{E}}\left[\bm{\theta}_{v,i}\otimes\bm{\theta}_{v,i}\right]\left(\sum_{j>i}^{T}\mathcal{A}^{j-i}\right)^{\top}\right\}.

Note that from here, a upper bound on
{\mathbb{E}}\left[\bm{\theta}_{v,i}\otimes\bm{\theta}_{v,i}\right]
doesnot translate to an upperbound on the
\mathbb{E}\left[{\overline{\bm{\theta}}_{v,T}}\otimes{\overline{\bm{\theta}}_{v,T}}\right]
as the matrix
\mathcal{A}
is not positive unlike the case of SGD. Using the following identity for any two matrices
S
and a vector
\phi
,

\phi\otimes\phi+S\cdot\phi\otimes\phi+\phi\otimes\phi\cdot S^{\top}=\left(I+S\right)\cdot\phi\otimes\phi\cdot\left(I+S\right)^{\top}-S\cdot\phi\otimes\phi\cdot S^{\top}.

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[{\overline{\bm{\theta}}_{v,T}}\otimes{\overline{\bm{\theta}}_{v,T}}\right]

\displaystyle=\sum_{i=1}^{T}\left(\mathcal{I}+\sum_{j>i}^{T}\mathcal{A}^{j-i}\right)\mathbb{E}\left[\bm{\theta}_{v,i}\otimes\bm{\theta}_{v,i}\right]\left(\mathcal{I}+\sum_{j>i}^{T}\mathcal{A}^{j-i}\right)^{\top}

\displaystyle\hskip 128.0374pt-\sum_{i=1}^{T}\left(\sum_{j>i}^{T}\mathcal{A}^{j-i}\right)\mathbb{E}\left[\bm{\theta}_{v,i}\otimes\bm{\theta}_{v,i}\right]\left(\sum_{j>i}^{T}\mathcal{A}^{j-i}\right)^{\top}.

Now the first term can be written as follows,

\displaystyle\sum_{i=1}^{T}\left(\mathcal{I}+\sum_{j>i}^{T}\mathcal{A}^{j-i}\right)\mathbb{E}\left[\bm{\theta}_{v,i}\otimes\bm{\theta}_{v,i}\right]\left(\mathcal{I}+\sum_{j>i}^{T}\mathcal{A}^{j-i}\right)^{\top}

\displaystyle=\sum_{i=1}^{T}\left(\sum_{j\geq i}^{T}\mathcal{A}^{j-i}\right)\mathbb{E}\left[\bm{\theta}_{v,i}\otimes\bm{\theta}_{v,i}\right]\left(\sum_{j>i}^{T}\mathcal{A}^{j-i}\right)^{\top}.

For the second term note that at
i=T
the summation will be
0
. So we directly consider the summation till
T-1
.

\displaystyle\sum_{i=1}^{T}\left(\sum_{j>i}^{T}\mathcal{A}^{j-i}\right)\mathbb{E}\left[\bm{\theta}_{v,i}\otimes\bm{\theta}_{v,i}\right]\left(\sum_{j>i}^{T}\mathcal{A}^{j-i}\right)^{\top}

\displaystyle=\sum_{i=1}^{T-1}\left(\sum_{j>i}^{T}\mathcal{A}^{j-i}\right)\mathbb{E}\left[\bm{\theta}_{v,i}\otimes\bm{\theta}_{v,i}\right]\left(\sum_{j>i}^{T}\mathcal{A}^{j-i}\right)^{\top},

\displaystyle=\sum_{i=1}^{T-1}\left(\sum_{j\geq i+1}^{T}\mathcal{A}^{j-i-1}\right)\mathcal{A}\mathbb{E}\left[\bm{\theta}_{v,i}\otimes\bm{\theta}_{v,i}\right]\mathcal{A}^{\top}\left(\sum_{j\geq i+1}^{T}\mathcal{A}^{j-i-1}\right)^{\top}.

By definition of
\widetilde{\mathcal{T}}
and change of variable ’
i+1\rightarrow i
’ gives

\displaystyle\sum_{i=1}^{T}\left(\sum_{j>i}^{T}\mathcal{A}^{j-i}\right)\mathbb{E}\left[\bm{\theta}_{v,i}\otimes\bm{\theta}_{v,i}\right]\left(\sum_{j>i}^{T}\mathcal{A}^{j-i}\right)^{\top}

\displaystyle=\sum_{i=2}^{T}\left(\sum_{j\geq i}^{T}\mathcal{A}^{j-i}\right)\widetilde{\mathcal{T}}\circ\mathbb{E}\left[\bm{\theta}_{v,i-1}\otimes\bm{\theta}_{v,i-1}\right]\left(\sum_{j\geq i}^{T}\mathcal{A}^{j-i}\right)^{\top}.

Combining both parts and noting that
\bm{\theta}_{v,0}=0
we get,

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[{\overline{\bm{\theta}}_{v,T}}\otimes{\overline{\bm{\theta}}_{v,T}}\right]=\sum_{i=1}^{T}\left(\sum_{j\geq i}^{T}\mathcal{A}^{j-i}\right)\left\{\mathbb{E}\left[\bm{\theta}_{v,i}\otimes\bm{\theta}_{v,i}\right]-\widetilde{\mathcal{T}}\circ\mathbb{E}\left[\bm{\theta}_{v,i-1}\otimes\bm{\theta}_{v,i-1}\right]\right\}\left(\sum_{j\geq i}^{T}\mathcal{A}^{j-i}\right)^{\top}.

From Lemma
14
,

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}\left[\bm{\theta}_{v,i}\otimes\bm{\theta}_{v,i}\right]

\displaystyle=\mathcal{T}\circ{\mathbb{E}}\left[\bm{\theta}_{v,i-1}\otimes\bm{\theta}_{v,i-1}\right]+{\mathbb{E}}\left[\bm{\epsilon_{i}}\otimes\bm{\epsilon_{i}}\right],

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}\left[\bm{\theta}_{v,i}\otimes\bm{\theta}_{v,i}\right]

\displaystyle=\left(\widetilde{\mathcal{T}}+{\mathcal{M}}\right)\circ{\mathbb{E}}\left[\bm{\theta}_{v,i-1}\otimes\bm{\theta}_{v,i-1}\right]+{\mathbb{E}}\left[\bm{\epsilon_{i}}\otimes\bm{\epsilon_{i}}\right],

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}\left[\bm{\theta}_{v,i}\otimes\bm{\theta}_{v,i}\right]-\widetilde{\mathcal{T}}\circ{\mathbb{E}}\left[\bm{\theta}_{v,i-1}\otimes\bm{\theta}_{v,i-1}\right]

\displaystyle={\mathcal{M}}\circ{\mathbb{E}}\left[\bm{\theta}_{v,i-1}\otimes\bm{\theta}_{v,i-1}\right]+{\mathbb{E}}\left[\bm{\epsilon_{i}}\otimes\bm{\epsilon_{i}}\right].

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[{\overline{\bm{\theta}}_{v,T}}\otimes{\overline{\bm{\theta}}_{v,T}}\right]

\displaystyle=\sum_{i=1}^{T}\left(\sum_{j\geq i}^{T}\mathcal{A}^{j-i}\right)\left\{\mathbb{E}\left[\bm{\theta}_{v,i}\otimes\bm{\theta}_{v,i}\right]-\widetilde{\mathcal{T}}\circ\mathbb{E}\left[\bm{\theta}_{v,i-1}\otimes\bm{\theta}_{v,i-1}\right]\right\}\left(\sum_{j\geq i}^{T}\mathcal{A}^{j-i}\right)^{\top},

\displaystyle=\sum_{i=1}^{T}\left(\sum_{j\geq i}^{T}\mathcal{A}^{j-i}\right)\left\{{\mathcal{M}}\circ{\mathbb{E}}\left[\bm{\theta}_{v,i-1}\otimes\bm{\theta}_{v,i-1}\right]+{\mathbb{E}}\left[\bm{\epsilon_{i}}\otimes\bm{\epsilon_{i}}\right]\right\}\left(\sum_{j\geq i}^{T}\mathcal{A}^{j-i}\right)^{\top}.

This proves the lemma.

Lemma 25

With the recursion defined by Eq.(
42
) and step sizes satisfying Condition
27
, for
t\geq 0
,

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}\left[\bm{\theta}_{v,t}\otimes\bm{\theta}_{v,t}\right]\preccurlyeq t^{2}\sigma^{2}\left(\mathcal{I}-\mathcal{T}\right)^{-1}\circ\bm{\Xi}.

Proof
From Lemma
14
, we have

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}\left[\bm{\theta}_{v,t}\otimes\bm{\theta}_{v,t}\right]

\displaystyle=\mathcal{T}\circ{\mathbb{E}}\left[\bm{\theta}_{v,t-1}\otimes\bm{\theta}_{v,t-1}\right]+{\mathbb{E}}\left[\bm{\epsilon_{t}}\otimes\bm{\epsilon_{t}}\right],

\displaystyle=\mathcal{T}^{2}\circ{\mathbb{E}}\left[\bm{\theta}_{v,t-2}\otimes\bm{\theta}_{v,t-2}\right]+\mathcal{T}\circ{\mathbb{E}}\left[\bm{\epsilon_{t-1}}\otimes\bm{\epsilon_{t-1}}\right]+{\mathbb{E}}\left[\bm{\epsilon_{t}}\otimes\bm{\epsilon_{t}}\right],

\displaystyle=\sum_{k=0}^{t-1}\mathcal{T}^{k}\circ{\mathbb{E}}\left[\bm{\epsilon_{t-k}}\otimes\bm{\epsilon_{t-k}}\right].

Recalling from the definition of
\bm{\epsilon_{k}}
and its covariance,

\displaystyle\bm{\epsilon_{k}}

\displaystyle=k\eta_{k}\begin{bmatrix}\beta a_{t}\\
\alpha a_{t}\end{bmatrix},

\displaystyle\bm{\epsilon_{k}}\otimes\bm{\epsilon_{k}}

\displaystyle=k^{2}\eta_{k}^{2}\begin{bmatrix}\beta a_{t}\\
\alpha a_{t}\end{bmatrix}\otimes\begin{bmatrix}\beta a_{t}\\
\alpha a_{t}\end{bmatrix},

\displaystyle=k^{2}\begin{bmatrix}\beta^{2}&\alpha\beta\\
\alpha\beta&\alpha^{2}\end{bmatrix}\otimes_{k}\left[\eta_{k}^{2}~~a_{k}\otimes a_{k}\right].

where
\otimes_{k}
is the kronecker product. Taking the expectation, we have

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}\left[\bm{\epsilon_{k}}\otimes\bm{\epsilon_{k}}\right]

\displaystyle=k^{2}\begin{bmatrix}\beta^{2}&\alpha\beta\\
\alpha\beta&\alpha^{2}\end{bmatrix}\otimes_{k}{\mathbb{E}}\left[\eta_{k}^{2}~~a_{k}\otimes a_{k}\right].

From the Assumption
2
, we have

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}\left[\eta_{k}^{2}~~a_{k}\otimes a_{k}\right]={\mathbb{E}}\left[\left(b_{k}-\left\langle{\bm{x}_{*}},{a_{k}}\right\rangle\right)^{2}a_{k}\otimes a_{k}\right]\preccurlyeq\sigma^{2}\mathbf{H}.

Using the fact that kronecker product of two PSD matrices is a PSD and recalling
\bm{\Xi}
from Eq.(
31
), we get

\displaystyle\begin{bmatrix}\beta^{2}&\alpha\beta\\
\alpha\beta&\alpha^{2}\end{bmatrix}\otimes_{k}\left(\sigma^{2}\mathbf{H}-{\mathbb{E}}\left[\eta_{k}^{2}~~a_{k}\otimes a_{k}\right]\right)

\displaystyle\succcurlyeq 0,

\displaystyle\begin{bmatrix}\beta^{2}&\alpha\beta\\
\alpha\beta&\alpha^{2}\end{bmatrix}\otimes_{k}\left({\mathbb{E}}\left[\eta_{k}^{2}~~a_{k}\otimes a_{k}\right]\right)

\displaystyle\preccurlyeq\begin{bmatrix}\beta^{2}&\alpha\beta\\
\alpha\beta&\alpha^{2}\end{bmatrix}\otimes_{k}\left(\sigma^{2}\mathbf{H}\right),

\displaystyle\preccurlyeq\sigma^{2}{\begin{bmatrix}\beta^{2}\mathbf{H}&\alpha\beta\mathbf{H}\\
\alpha\beta\mathbf{H}&\alpha^{2}\mathbf{H}\end{bmatrix}}=\sigma^{2}\bm{\Xi}.

Combining these we get the following,

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}\left[\bm{\epsilon_{k}}\otimes\bm{\epsilon_{k}}\right]\preccurlyeq\sigma^{2}k^{2}\cdot\bm{\Xi}.

Using this upper bound in the expansion of
{\mathbb{E}}\left[\bm{\theta}_{v,t}\otimes\bm{\theta}_{v,t}\right]
,

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}\left[\bm{\theta}_{v,t}\otimes\bm{\theta}_{v,t}\right]

\displaystyle=\sum_{k=0}^{t-1}\mathcal{T}^{k}\circ{\mathbb{E}}\left[\bm{\epsilon_{t-k}}\otimes\bm{\epsilon_{t-k}}\right],

\displaystyle\preccurlyeq\sigma^{2}\sum_{k=0}^{t-1}\mathcal{T}^{k}\circ(t-k)^{2}~\bm{\Xi}.

For
0\leqslant k\leqslant T
, we have
(t-k)^{2}\leqslant t^{2}
and using the fact that
\mathcal{T},\bm{\Xi}
are positive,

\displaystyle\sum_{k=0}^{t-1}\mathcal{T}^{k}\circ(t-k)^{2}~\bm{\Xi}

\displaystyle\preccurlyeq t^{2}\sum_{k=0}^{t-1}\mathcal{T}^{k}\circ\bm{\Xi},

\displaystyle\preccurlyeq t^{2}\sum_{k=0}^{\infty}\mathcal{T}^{k}\circ\bm{\Xi}~=~t^{2}\left(\mathcal{I}-\mathcal{T}\right)^{-1}\circ\bm{\Xi}.

Hence, we have

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}\left[\bm{\theta}_{v,t}\otimes\bm{\theta}_{v,t}\right]\preccurlyeq\sigma^{2}t^{2}\left(\mathcal{I}-\mathcal{T}\right)^{-1}\circ\bm{\Xi}

This completes the proof of the lemma.

Lemma 26

With
\alpha
and
\beta
satisfying Condition
27
, the excess error after
T
iterations of the variance process,

\displaystyle\left\langle{\begin{bmatrix}\mathbf{H}&\mathbf{H}\\
\mathbf{H}&\mathbf{H}\end{bmatrix}},{\mathbb{E}\left[{\overline{\bm{\theta}}_{v,T}}\otimes{\overline{\bm{\theta}}_{v,T}}\right]}\right\rangle

\displaystyle\leq 18\left(\sigma^{2}d\right)T^{3}

Proof

From Lemma
24
,

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[{\overline{\bm{\theta}}_{v,T}}\otimes{\overline{\bm{\theta}}_{v,T}}\right]

\displaystyle=\sum_{i=1}^{T}\left(\sum_{j\geq i}^{T}\mathcal{A}^{j-i}\right)\left\{{\mathcal{M}}\circ{\mathbb{E}}\left[\bm{\theta}_{v,i-1}\otimes\bm{\theta}_{v,i-1}\right]+{\mathbb{E}}\left[\bm{\epsilon_{i}}\otimes\bm{\epsilon_{i}}\right]\right\}\left(\sum_{j\geq i}^{T}\mathcal{A}^{j-i}\right)^{\top}.

First lets upperbound
{\mathcal{M}}\circ{\mathbb{E}}\left[\bm{\theta}_{v,i-1}\otimes\bm{\theta}_{v,i-1}\right]+{\mathbb{E}}\left[\bm{\epsilon_{i}}\otimes\bm{\epsilon_{i}}\right]
. We have the following

•

Invoking Lemma
25
,

{\mathbb{E}}\left[\bm{\theta}_{v,i-1}\otimes\bm{\theta}_{v,i-1}\right]\preccurlyeq(i-1)^{2}\sigma^{2}\left(\mathcal{I}-\mathcal{T}\right)^{-1}\circ\bm{\Xi}.

•

For the choice of stepsizes from Lemma
30
,

\left(\mathcal{I}-\mathcal{T}\right)^{-1}\circ\bm{\Xi}\preccurlyeq 3\left(\mathcal{I}-\widetilde{\mathcal{T}}\right)^{-1}\circ\bm{\Xi}.

•

Combining these to get

{\mathcal{M}}\circ{\mathbb{E}}\left[\bm{\theta}_{v,i-1}\otimes\bm{\theta}_{v,i-1}\right]\preccurlyeq 3(i-1)^{2}\sigma^{2}{\mathcal{M}}\circ\left(\mathcal{I}-\widetilde{\mathcal{T}}\right)^{-1}\circ\bm{\Xi}.

the step sizes chosen allows us to invoke Lemma
33
. Hence,

\displaystyle{\mathcal{M}}\circ\left(\mathcal{I}-\widetilde{\mathcal{T}}\right)^{-1}\circ\bm{\Xi}

\displaystyle\preccurlyeq\frac{2}{3}\bm{\Xi},

\displaystyle 3\sigma^{2}(i-1)^{2}{\mathcal{M}}\circ\left(\mathcal{I}-\widetilde{\mathcal{T}}\right)^{-1}\circ\bm{\Xi}

\displaystyle\preccurlyeq 2\sigma^{2}(i-1)^{2}\bm{\Xi}.

•

The remaining
{\mathbb{E}}\left[\bm{\epsilon_{i}}\otimes\bm{\epsilon_{i}}\right]
can be upperbounded by
\sigma^{2}i^{2}\bm{\Xi}
.

Combining the above gives

\displaystyle{\mathcal{M}}\circ{\mathbb{E}}\left[\bm{\theta}_{v,i-1}\otimes\bm{\theta}_{v,i-1}\right]+{\mathbb{E}}\left[\bm{\epsilon_{i}}\otimes\bm{\epsilon_{i}}\right]

\displaystyle\preccurlyeq 2\sigma^{2}(i-1)^{2}\bm{\Xi}+\sigma^{2}i^{2}\bm{\Xi}.

For
0\leq i\leq T
this can be bounded as follows.

\displaystyle{\mathcal{M}}\circ{\mathbb{E}}\left[\bm{\theta}_{v,i-1}\otimes\bm{\theta}_{v,i-1}\right]+{\mathbb{E}}\left[\bm{\epsilon_{i}}\otimes\bm{\epsilon_{i}}\right]

\displaystyle\preccurlyeq 3\sigma^{2}T^{2}\bm{\Xi}.

Note that this can be used in Lemma
24
to bound
\mathbb{E}\left[{\overline{\bm{\theta}}_{v,T}}\otimes{\overline{\bm{\theta}}_{v,T}}\right]
because for any matrix P,
P(.)P^{\top}
is a positive operator. Hence

\displaystyle\left(\sum_{j\geq i}^{T}\mathcal{A}^{j-i}\right)\left\{{\mathcal{M}}\circ{\mathbb{E}}\left[\bm{\theta}_{v,i-1}\otimes\bm{\theta}_{v,i-1}\right]+{\mathbb{E}}\left[\bm{\epsilon_{i}}\otimes\bm{\epsilon_{i}}\right]\right\}\left(\sum_{j\geq i}^{T}\mathcal{A}^{j-i}\right)^{\top}

\displaystyle\preccurlyeq 3\sigma^{2}T^{2}\left(\sum_{j\geq i}^{T}\mathcal{A}^{j-i}\right)\cdot\bm{\Xi}\cdot\left(\sum_{j\geq i}^{T}\mathcal{A}^{j-i}\right)^{\top}.

Adding this and using Lemma
24
,

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[{\overline{\bm{\theta}}_{v,T}}\otimes{\overline{\bm{\theta}}_{v,T}}\right]

\displaystyle\preccurlyeq 3\sigma^{2}T^{2}\cdot\sum_{i=1}^{T}\left(\sum_{j\geq i}^{T}\mathcal{A}^{j-i}\right)\bm{\Xi}\left(\sum_{j\geq i}^{T}\mathcal{A}^{j-i}\right)^{\top}.

Using the following identity,

\displaystyle\left(\sum_{j\geq i}^{T}\mathcal{A}^{j-i}\right)

\displaystyle=\left(\mathcal{I}-\mathcal{A}\right)^{-1}\left(\mathcal{I}-\mathcal{A}^{\left(T-i+1\right)}\right),

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[{\overline{\bm{\theta}}_{v,T}}\otimes{\overline{\bm{\theta}}_{v,T}}\right]

\displaystyle\preccurlyeq 3\sigma^{2}T^{2}\cdot\left(\mathcal{I}-\mathcal{A}\right)^{-1}\left[\sum_{i=1}^{T}\left(\mathcal{I}-\mathcal{A}^{\left(T-i+1\right)}\right)\bm{\Xi}\left(\mathcal{I}-\mathcal{A}^{\left(T-i+1\right)}\right)^{\top}\right]\left(\mathcal{I}-\mathcal{A}^{\top}\right)^{-1}.

Note that we are interested in
\left\langle{\begin{bmatrix}\mathbf{H}&\mathbf{H}\\
\mathbf{H}&\mathbf{H}\end{bmatrix}},{\mathbb{E}\left[{\overline{\bm{\theta}}_{v,T}}\otimes{\overline{\bm{\theta}}_{v,T}}\right]}\right\rangle.

\displaystyle\left\langle{\begin{bmatrix}\mathbf{H}&\mathbf{H}\\
\mathbf{H}&\mathbf{H}\end{bmatrix}},{\mathbb{E}\left[{\overline{\bm{\theta}}_{v,T}}\otimes{\overline{\bm{\theta}}_{v,T}}\right]}\right\rangle

\displaystyle\hskip-71.13188pt\leq 3\sigma^{2}T^{2}\left\langle{\begin{bmatrix}\mathbf{H}&\mathbf{H}\\
\mathbf{H}&\mathbf{H}\end{bmatrix}},{\left(\mathcal{I}-\mathcal{A}\right)^{-1}\left[\sum_{i=1}^{T}\left(\mathcal{I}-\mathcal{A}^{\left(T-i+1\right)}\right)\bm{\Xi}\left(\mathcal{I}-\mathcal{A}^{\left(T-i+1\right)}\right)^{\top}\right]\left(\mathcal{I}-\mathcal{A}^{\top}\right)^{-1}}\right\rangle,

\displaystyle\hskip-71.13188pt=3\sigma^{2}T^{2}\left\langle{\left(\mathcal{I}-\mathcal{A}^{\top}\right)^{-1}\begin{bmatrix}\mathbf{H}&\mathbf{H}\\
\mathbf{H}&\mathbf{H}\end{bmatrix}\left(\mathcal{I}-\mathcal{A}\right)^{-1}},{\sum_{i=1}^{T}\left(\mathcal{I}-\mathcal{A}^{\left(T-i+1\right)}\right)\bm{\Xi}\left(\mathcal{I}-\mathcal{A}^{\left(T-i+1\right)}\right)^{\top}}\right\rangle.

Note that

\displaystyle\left(\mathcal{I}-\mathcal{A}\right)^{-1}

\displaystyle=\begin{bmatrix}\mathbf{I}&\left(\alpha\mathbf{H}\right)^{-1}\left(\mathbf{I}-\beta\mathbf{H}\right)\\
-\mathbf{I}&\left(\alpha\mathbf{H}\right)^{-1}\left(\beta\mathbf{H}\right)\\
\end{bmatrix},

\displaystyle\begin{bmatrix}\mathbf{H}&\mathbf{H}\\
\mathbf{H}&\mathbf{H}\end{bmatrix}\left(\mathcal{I}-\mathcal{A}\right)^{-1}

\displaystyle=\begin{bmatrix}\mathbf{H}&\mathbf{H}\\
\mathbf{H}&\mathbf{H}\end{bmatrix}\begin{bmatrix}\mathbf{I}&\left(\alpha\mathbf{H}\right)^{-1}\left(\mathbf{I}-\beta\mathbf{H}\right)\\
-\mathbf{I}&\left(\alpha\mathbf{H}\right)^{-1}\left(\beta\mathbf{H}\right)\\
\end{bmatrix},

\displaystyle=\begin{bmatrix}0&\alpha^{\scriptscriptstyle-1}\mathbf{I}\\
0&\alpha^{\scriptscriptstyle-1}\mathbf{I}\end{bmatrix},

\displaystyle\left(\mathcal{I}-\mathcal{A}^{\top}\right)^{-1}\begin{bmatrix}\mathbf{H}&\mathbf{H}\\
\mathbf{H}&\mathbf{H}\end{bmatrix}\left(\mathcal{I}-\mathcal{A}\right)^{-1}

\displaystyle=\begin{bmatrix}\mathbf{I}&-\mathbf{I}\\
\left(\alpha\mathbf{H}\right)^{-1}\left(\mathbf{I}-\beta\mathbf{H}\right)&\left(\alpha\mathbf{H}\right)^{-1}\left(\beta\mathbf{H}\right)\\
\end{bmatrix}\begin{bmatrix}0&\alpha^{\scriptscriptstyle-1}\mathbf{I}\\
0&\alpha^{\scriptscriptstyle-1}\mathbf{I}\end{bmatrix}=\begin{bmatrix}0&0\\
0&\alpha^{\scriptscriptstyle-2}\mathbf{H}^{-1}\end{bmatrix}.

Substituting this,

\displaystyle\left\langle{\begin{bmatrix}\mathbf{H}&\mathbf{H}\\
\mathbf{H}&\mathbf{H}\end{bmatrix}},{\mathbb{E}\left[{\overline{\bm{\theta}}_{v,T}}\otimes{\overline{\bm{\theta}}_{v,T}}\right]}\right\rangle

\displaystyle\leq 3\sigma^{2}T^{2}\left\langle{\begin{bmatrix}0&0\\
0&\alpha^{\scriptscriptstyle-2}\mathbf{H}^{-1}\end{bmatrix}},{\sum_{i=1}^{T}\left(\mathcal{I}-\mathcal{A}^{\left(T-i+1\right)}\right)\bm{\Xi}\left(\mathcal{I}-\mathcal{A}^{\left(T-i+1\right)}\right)^{\top}}\right\rangle,

\displaystyle=3\sigma^{2}T^{2}\sum_{i=1}^{T}\left\langle{\begin{bmatrix}0&0\\
0&\alpha^{\scriptscriptstyle-2}\mathbf{H}^{-1}\end{bmatrix}},{\left(\mathcal{I}-\mathcal{A}^{\left(i\right)}\right)\bm{\Xi}\left(\mathcal{I}-\mathcal{A}^{\left(i\right)}\right)^{\top}}\right\rangle.

From Cauchy Schwarz, we know

\displaystyle\left(\mathcal{I}-\mathcal{A}^{\left(i\right)}\right)\bm{\Xi}\left(\mathcal{I}-\mathcal{A}^{\left(i\right)}\right)^{\top}

\displaystyle\preccurlyeq 2\bm{\Xi}+2\mathcal{A}^{\left(i\right)}\bm{\Xi}\left(\mathcal{A}^{\top}\right)^{\left(i\right)}=2\bm{\Xi}+2\widetilde{\mathcal{T}}^{i}\circ\bm{\Xi},

Using this,

\displaystyle\left\langle{\begin{bmatrix}\mathbf{H}&\mathbf{H}\\
\mathbf{H}&\mathbf{H}\end{bmatrix}},{\mathbb{E}\left[{\overline{\bm{\theta}}_{v,T}}\otimes{\overline{\bm{\theta}}_{v,T}}\right]}\right\rangle

\displaystyle\leq 3\sigma^{2}T^{2}\sum_{i=1}^{T}\left\langle{\begin{bmatrix}0&0\\
0&\alpha^{\scriptscriptstyle-2}\mathbf{H}^{-1}\end{bmatrix}},{2\bm{\Xi}+2\widetilde{\mathcal{T}}^{i}\circ\bm{\Xi}}\right\rangle.

Using Lemma
28
for the right part, we get the following,

\displaystyle\left\langle{\begin{bmatrix}\mathbf{H}&\mathbf{H}\\
\mathbf{H}&\mathbf{H}\end{bmatrix}},{\mathbb{E}\left[{\overline{\bm{\theta}}_{v,T}}\otimes{\overline{\bm{\theta}}_{v,T}}\right]}\right\rangle

\displaystyle\leq 3\sigma^{2}T^{2}\sum_{i=1}^{T}6d=18\left(\sigma^{2}d\right)T^{3}.

C.5
Potentials for Nesterov Method

In this section, we will use the potential functions used in the proof of nesterov accelerated method to bounding the terms in our recursion. Consider the Algorithm
4
with exact gradients in that setting,

\displaystyle\bm{y}_{t+1}^{\prime}=\bm{x}_{t}^{\prime}-\beta\mathbf{H}\left(\bm{x}_{t}^{\prime}-\bm{x}_{*}\right),

(44a)

\displaystyle\bm{z}_{t+1}^{\prime}=\bm{z}_{t}^{\prime}-\alpha(t+1)\mathbf{H}\left(\bm{x}_{t}^{\prime}-\bm{x}_{*}\right),

(44b)

\displaystyle(t+2)\bm{x}_{t+1}^{\prime}=(t+1)\bm{y}_{t+1}^{\prime}+\bm{z}_{t+1}^{\prime}.

(44c)

By similar rescaling and the definition of the operator, it can be seen that

\displaystyle\begin{bmatrix}t(\bm{y}_{t}^{\prime}-\bm{x}_{*})\\
\bm{z}_{t}^{\prime}-\bm{x}_{*}\end{bmatrix}=\mathcal{A}^{t}\circ\begin{bmatrix}0\\
\bm{x}_{0}-\bm{x}_{*}.\end{bmatrix}

(45)

Lemma 27

For the step sizes satisfying
0<\alpha\leq\beta\leq 1/L
,

\displaystyle\left\langle{\begin{bmatrix}\mathbf{H}&\mathbf{H}\\
\mathbf{H}&\mathbf{H}\end{bmatrix}},{\widetilde{\mathcal{T}}^{~\left(t\right)}\circ\left[\bm{\theta}_{0}\otimes\bm{\theta}_{0}\right]}\right\rangle\leq\min\left\{\frac{1}{\alpha},\frac{8(t+1)}{\beta}\right\}\bigl\|{\bm{x}_{0}-\bm{x}_{*}}\bigr\|^{2}.

Proof

From the above equivalence Eq.(
45
), we can see that

\displaystyle\left\langle{\begin{bmatrix}\mathbf{H}&\mathbf{H}\\
\mathbf{H}&\mathbf{H}\end{bmatrix}},{\widetilde{\mathcal{T}}^{~\left(t\right)}\circ\left[\bm{\theta}_{0}\otimes\bm{\theta}_{0}\right]}\right\rangle

\displaystyle=(t+1)^{2}\bigl\|{\bm{x}_{t}^{\prime}-\bm{x}_{0}}\bigr\|^{2}.

Now using the potential function,

V_{t}=(t)(t+1)\bigl\|{\bm{y}_{t}^{\prime}-\bm{x}_{*}}\bigr\|^{2}_{\mathbf{H}}+\frac{1}{a}\bigl\|{\bm{z}_{t}^{\prime}-\bm{x}_{*}}\bigr\|^{2}
, for
\alpha\leq\beta
we can see that
V_{t}\leq V_{t-1}\leq\ldots V_{0}
. Using this

\displaystyle t^{2}\bigl\|{\bm{y}_{t}^{\prime}-\bm{x}_{*}}\bigr\|^{2}\leq\frac{1}{a}\bigl\|{\bm{z}_{0}^{\prime}-\bm{x}_{*}}\bigr\|^{2}=\frac{1}{a}\bigl\|{\bm{x}_{0}-\bm{x}_{*}}\bigr\|^{2},

\displaystyle\bigl\|{\bm{z}_{t}^{\prime}-\bm{x}_{*}}\bigr\|^{2}\leq\bigl\|{\bm{z}_{0}^{\prime}-\bm{x}_{*}}\bigr\|^{2}=\bigl\|{\bm{x}_{0}-\bm{x}_{*}}\bigr\|^{2}.

Noting that
(t+1)(\bm{x}_{t}^{\prime}-\bm{x}_{*})=t(\bm{y}_{t}^{\prime}-\bm{x}_{*})+(\bm{z}_{t}^{\prime}-\bm{x}_{*})
and using Cauchy-Schwarz inequality,

\displaystyle(t+1)^{2}\bigl\|{\bm{x}_{t}^{\prime}-\bm{x}_{0}}\bigr\|^{2}\leq 2t^{2}\bigl\|{\bm{y}_{t}^{\prime}-\bm{x}_{*}}\bigr\|^{2}+2\bigl\|{\bm{z}_{t}^{\prime}-\bm{x}_{*}}\bigr\|^{2}=\left(\frac{2}{\alpha}+2\right)\bigl\|{\bm{x}_{0}-\bm{x}_{*}}\bigr\|^{2}.

But doing exact computations gives better bounds. The above algorithm is exactly equivalent to the algorithm considered in
Flammarion and Bach 2015
as seen below

\displaystyle\eta_{t+1}=(\mathbf{I}-\alpha\mathbf{H})\eta_{t}+(\mathbf{I}-\beta\mathbf{H})(\eta_{t}-\eta_{t-1}).

where
\eta_{t}=(t+1)(\bm{x}_{t}^{\prime}-\bm{x}_{*})
. Hence we can apply their results giving the bound
\min\left\{\frac{1}{\alpha},\frac{8(t+1)}{\beta}\right\}\bigl\|{\bm{x}_{0}-\bm{x}_{*}}\bigr\|^{2}
.

Lemma 28

\displaystyle\left\langle{\begin{bmatrix}0&0\\
0&\alpha^{\scriptscriptstyle-2}\mathbf{H}^{-1}\end{bmatrix}},{\widetilde{\mathcal{T}}^{~\left(t\right)}\circ\bm{\Xi}}\right\rangle\leq 2.

Proof

Both
\mathcal{A}
and
\bm{\Xi}
are diagonizable wrt to the eigen basis of
\mathbf{H}
. We will now project these block matrices onto their eigen basis and compute the summation of each component individually. Note that,

\displaystyle\mathcal{A}=\sum_{i=1}^{d}\mathcal{A}_{i}\otimes_{k}e_{i}e_{i}^{\top},\qquad\bm{\Xi}=\sum_{i=1}^{d}\bm{\Xi}_{i}\otimes_{k}e_{i}e_{i}^{\top}.

(46)

where
\mathcal{A}_{i}
and
\bm{\Xi}_{i}
are

\displaystyle\mathcal{A}_{i}=\begin{bmatrix}1-\beta\lambda_{i}&1-\beta\lambda_{i}\\
-\alpha\lambda_{i}&1-\alpha\lambda_{i}\end{bmatrix},\qquad\bm{\Xi}_{i}=\begin{bmatrix}\beta^{2}\lambda_{i}&\beta\alpha\lambda_{i}\\
\beta\alpha\lambda_{i}&\alpha^{2}\lambda_{i}\end{bmatrix}.

(47)

Now, the scalar product using the properties of Kronecker product,

\displaystyle\left\langle{\begin{bmatrix}0&0\\
0&\alpha^{\scriptscriptstyle-2}\mathbf{H}^{-1}\end{bmatrix}},{\widetilde{\mathcal{T}}^{~\left(t\right)}\circ\bm{\Xi}}\right\rangle=\sum_{i=1}^{d}\left\langle{\begin{bmatrix}0&0\\
0&\alpha^{-2}\lambda_{i}^{-1}\end{bmatrix}},{\mathcal{A}_{i}^{t}\cdot\bm{\Xi}_{i}\cdot\left(\mathcal{A}_{i}^{t}\right)^{\top}}\right\rangle.

To compute
\left\langle{\begin{bmatrix}0&0\\
0&\alpha^{-2}\lambda_{i}^{-1}\end{bmatrix}},{\mathcal{A}_{i}^{t}\cdot\bm{\Xi}_{i}\cdot\left(\mathcal{A}_{i}^{t}\right)^{\top}}\right\rangle
we invoke Lemma
67
, with

\displaystyle\Gamma=\mathcal{A}_{i},

\displaystyle\qquad\aleph=\lambda_{i}\bm{\Xi}_{i},

\displaystyle b=\beta\lambda_{i},

\displaystyle\qquad a=\alpha\lambda_{i}.

which gives,

\displaystyle\left\langle{\begin{bmatrix}0&0\\
0&\alpha^{-2}\lambda_{i}^{-1}\end{bmatrix}},{\mathcal{A}_{i}^{t}\cdot\bm{\Xi}_{i}\cdot\left(\mathcal{A}_{i}^{t}\right)^{\top}}\right\rangle

\displaystyle\leq 1+\frac{\alpha\lambda_{i}}{\left(1-\beta\lambda_{i}\right)^{2}}.

Note from condition on step sizes Eq.(
27
) that
\alpha\leq\beta/2,\beta\lambda_{i}\leq\beta L\leq\beta R^{2}\leq 1/2
,

\displaystyle\left\langle{\begin{bmatrix}0&0\\
0&\alpha^{-2}\lambda_{i}^{-1}\end{bmatrix}},{\mathcal{A}_{i}^{t}\cdot\bm{\Xi}_{i}\cdot\left(\mathcal{A}_{i}^{t}\right)^{\top}}\right\rangle

\displaystyle\leq 1+\frac{\alpha\lambda_{i}}{\left(1-\beta\lambda_{i}\right)^{2}}\leq 2.

Computing the sum across dimension, we get the desired bound.

Appendix D
Inverting operators

In this section, we give proof for the almost eigenvalues of the operators
{\mathcal{M}}\circ(1-\widetilde{\mathcal{T}})^{-1}
,
{\mathcal{M}}^{\top}\circ\left(\mathcal{I}-\widetilde{\mathcal{T}}^{\top}\right)^{-1}
. As described earlier, although the calculations are a bit extensive, the underlying scheme remains the same. To compute
(1-\widetilde{\mathcal{T}})^{-1},\left(\mathcal{I}-\widetilde{\mathcal{T}}^{\top}\right)^{-1}
, we formulate inverse as a summation of geometric series. Then we use the diagonalization of the
\mathbf{H}
and compute the geometric series. In the last part, we use Property
1
and Assumptions
1
,
3
on the data features to get the final bounds.

Property 1

Using
{\mathbb{E}}\left[aa^{\top}\right]=\mathbf{H}
, the following property holds for any PSD matrix
(\cdot)
,

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}\left[\left(\mathbf{H}-aa^{\top}\right)(\cdot)\left(\mathbf{H}-aa^{\top}\right)\right]

\displaystyle={\mathbb{E}}\left[aa^{\top}(\cdot)aa^{\top}\right]-{\mathbb{E}}\left[\mathbf{H}(\cdot)\mathbf{H}\right]\preccurlyeq{\mathbb{E}}\left[aa^{\top}(\cdot)aa^{\top}\right].

Lemma 29

With
0<\alpha,\beta<1/L
and
(\alpha+2\beta)L<1
,

\displaystyle\left(\mathcal{I}-\widetilde{\mathcal{T}}\right)^{-1}\circ\bm{\Xi}

\displaystyle\preccurlyeq\frac{1}{3}\begin{bmatrix}2\alpha(\beta\mathbf{H})^{-1}+(2\beta-3\alpha)\mathbf{I}&\alpha\beta^{-1}(2\beta-\alpha)\mathbf{I}\\
\alpha\beta^{-1}(2\beta-\alpha)\mathbf{I}&2\alpha^{2}\beta^{-1}\mathbf{I}\end{bmatrix}.

(48)

Proof
We will compute the inverse by evaluating the summation of the following infinite series,

\displaystyle\left(\mathcal{I}-\widetilde{\mathcal{T}}\right)^{-1}\circ\bm{\Xi}=\sum_{t=0}^{\infty}\widetilde{\mathcal{T}}^{t}\circ\bm{\Xi}

\displaystyle=\sum_{t=0}^{\infty}\mathcal{A}^{t}\cdot\bm{\Xi}\cdot(\mathcal{A}^{t})^{\top}.

Both
\mathcal{A}
and
\bm{\Xi}
are diagonizable wrt to the eigen basis of
\mathbf{H}
. We will now project these block matrices onto their eigen basis and compute the summation of each component individually. Note that,

\displaystyle\mathcal{A}=\sum_{i}\mathcal{A}_{i}\otimes_{k}e_{i}e_{i}^{\top},\qquad\bm{\Xi}=\sum_{i}\bm{\Xi}_{i}\otimes_{k}e_{i}e_{i}^{\top}.

(49)

where
\mathcal{A}_{i}
and
\bm{\Xi}_{i}
are

\displaystyle\mathcal{A}_{i}=\begin{bmatrix}1-\beta\lambda_{i}&1-\beta\lambda_{i}\\
-\alpha\lambda_{i}&1-\alpha\lambda_{i}\end{bmatrix},\qquad\bm{\Xi}_{i}=\begin{bmatrix}\beta^{2}\lambda_{i}&\beta\alpha\lambda_{i}\\
\beta\alpha\lambda_{i}&\alpha^{2}\lambda_{i}\end{bmatrix}.

(50)

Using these projections,

\displaystyle\sum_{t=0}^{\infty}\mathcal{A}^{t}\cdot\bm{\Xi}\cdot(\mathcal{A}^{t})^{\top}

\displaystyle=\sum_{t=0}^{\infty}\sum_{i}\left(\mathcal{A}_{i}^{t}\cdot\bm{\Xi}_{i}\cdot\left(\mathcal{A}_{i}^{t}\right)^{\top}\right)\otimes_{k}e_{i}e_{i}^{\top},

(51)

\displaystyle=\sum_{i}\left[\sum_{t=0}^{\infty}\mathcal{A}_{i}^{t}\cdot\bm{\Xi}_{i}\cdot\left(\mathcal{A}_{i}^{t}\right)^{\top}\right]\otimes_{k}e_{i}e_{i}^{\top}.

(52)

We invoke Lemma
37
with

\displaystyle\Gamma=\mathcal{A}_{i},

\displaystyle\qquad\aleph=\lambda_{i}\bm{\Xi}_{i},

\displaystyle b=\beta\lambda_{i},

\displaystyle\qquad a=\alpha\lambda_{i}.

\displaystyle\sum_{t=0}^{\infty}\mathcal{A}_{i}^{t}\cdot\lambda_{i}\bm{\Xi}_{i}\cdot\left(\mathcal{A}_{i}^{t}\right)^{\top}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{\beta\lambda_{i}(4-(\alpha+2\beta)\lambda_{i})}\begin{bmatrix}2\alpha\lambda_{i}+\beta\lambda_{i}(2\beta\lambda_{i}-3\alpha\lambda_{i})&\alpha\lambda_{i}(2\beta\lambda_{i}-\alpha\lambda_{i})\\
\alpha\lambda_{i}(2\beta\lambda_{i}-\alpha\lambda_{i})&2(\alpha\lambda_{i})^{2}\end{bmatrix},

\displaystyle\sum_{t=0}^{\infty}\mathcal{A}_{i}^{t}\cdot\bm{\Xi}_{i}\cdot\left(\mathcal{A}_{i}^{t}\right)^{\top}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{(4-(\alpha+2\beta)\lambda_{i})}\begin{bmatrix}2\alpha(\beta\lambda_{i})^{-1}+(2\beta-3\alpha)&\alpha\beta^{-1}(2\beta-\alpha)\\
\alpha\beta^{-1}(2\beta-\alpha)&2\alpha^{2}\beta^{-1}\end{bmatrix}.

We have

\displaystyle(\alpha+2\beta)\lambda_{i}\leq(\alpha+2\beta)L\leq 1,

\displaystyle\text{Hence, }4-((\alpha+2\beta)\lambda_{i})\geq 3.

Also,
\sum\limits_{t=0}^{\infty}\mathcal{A}_{i}^{t}\cdot\bm{\Xi}_{i}\cdot\left(\mathcal{A}_{i}^{t}\right)^{\top}
is PSD as
\widetilde{\mathcal{T}},\bm{\Xi}
are positive. Hence, the following holds

\displaystyle 3\sum_{t=0}^{\infty}\mathcal{A}_{i}^{t}\cdot\bm{\Xi}_{i}\cdot\left(\mathcal{A}_{i}^{t}\right)^{\top}\preccurlyeq 4-((\alpha+2\beta)\lambda_{i})\sum_{t=0}^{\infty}\mathcal{A}_{i}^{t}\cdot\bm{\Xi}_{i}\cdot\left(\mathcal{A}_{i}^{t}\right)^{\top},

\displaystyle=\begin{bmatrix}2\alpha(\beta\lambda_{i})^{-1}+(2\beta-3\alpha)&\alpha\beta^{-1}(2\beta-\alpha)\\
\alpha\beta^{-1}(2\beta-\alpha)&2\alpha^{2}\beta^{-1}\end{bmatrix}.

This given the following

\displaystyle\sum_{t=0}^{\infty}\mathcal{A}_{i}^{t}\cdot\bm{\Xi}_{i}\cdot\left(\mathcal{A}_{i}^{t}\right)^{\top}\preccurlyeq\frac{1}{3}\begin{bmatrix}2\alpha(\beta\lambda_{i})^{-1}+(2\beta-3\alpha)&\alpha\beta^{-1}(2\beta-\alpha)\\
\alpha\beta^{-1}(2\beta-\alpha)&2\alpha^{2}\beta^{-1}\end{bmatrix}.

(53)

Using the fact that kronecker product of two PSD matrices is positive,

\displaystyle\sum_{t=0}^{\infty}\mathcal{A}_{i}^{t}\cdot\bm{\Xi}_{i}\cdot\left(\mathcal{A}_{i}^{t}\right)^{\top}\otimes_{k}e_{i}e_{i}^{\top}\preccurlyeq\frac{1}{3}\begin{bmatrix}2\alpha(\beta\lambda_{i})^{-1}+(2\beta-3\alpha)&\alpha\beta^{-1}(2\beta-\alpha)\\
\alpha\beta^{-1}(2\beta-\alpha)&2\alpha^{2}\beta^{-1}\end{bmatrix}\otimes_{k}e_{i}e_{i}^{\top}.

Now adding this result along all directions we get

\displaystyle\sum_{i}\sum_{t=0}^{\infty}\mathcal{A}_{i}^{t}\cdot\bm{\Xi}_{i}\cdot\left(\mathcal{A}_{i}^{t}\right)^{\top}\otimes_{k}e_{i}e_{i}^{\top}

\displaystyle\preccurlyeq\frac{1}{3}\sum_{i}\begin{bmatrix}2\alpha(\beta\lambda_{i})^{-1}+(2\beta-3\alpha)&\alpha\beta^{-1}(2\beta-\alpha)\\
\alpha\beta^{-1}(2\beta-\alpha)&2\alpha^{2}\beta^{-1}\end{bmatrix}\otimes_{k}e_{i}e_{i}^{\top},

\displaystyle\left(\mathcal{I}-\widetilde{\mathcal{T}}\right)^{-1}\circ\bm{\Xi}

\displaystyle\preccurlyeq\frac{1}{3}\begin{bmatrix}2\alpha(\beta\mathbf{H})^{-1}+(2\beta-3\alpha)\mathbf{I}&\alpha\beta^{-1}(2\beta-\alpha)\mathbf{I}\\
\alpha\beta^{-1}(2\beta-\alpha)\mathbf{I}&2\alpha^{2}\beta^{-1}\mathbf{I}\end{bmatrix},

where the last inequation comes from the facts

\displaystyle\mathbf{I}=\sum_{i}e_{i}e_{i}^{\top}\qquad H^{-1}=\sum_{i}\lambda_{i}^{-1}e_{i}e_{i}^{\top}.

Lemma 30

With
(\alpha+2\beta)R^{2}\leq 1,\alpha\leq\frac{\beta}{2\tilde{\kappa}}
,

\displaystyle\bm{\Phi}_{\scriptscriptstyle\infty}\overset{\scriptstyle\mathrm{def}}{=}\left(\mathcal{I}-\mathcal{T}\right)^{-1}\circ\bm{\Xi}

\displaystyle\preccurlyeq 3\cdot\left(\mathcal{I}-\widetilde{\mathcal{T}}\right)^{-1}\circ\bm{\Xi}=\begin{bmatrix}2\alpha(\beta\mathbf{H})^{-1}+(2\beta-3\alpha)\mathbf{I}&\alpha\beta^{-1}(2\beta-\alpha)\mathbf{I}\\
\alpha\beta^{-1}(2\beta-\alpha)\mathbf{I}&2\alpha^{2}\beta^{-1}\mathbf{I}\end{bmatrix}.

(54)

Proof

Writing the inverse as a sum of exponential series gives us

\displaystyle\bm{\Phi}_{\scriptscriptstyle\infty}\overset{\scriptstyle\mathrm{def}}{=}\left(\mathcal{I}-\mathcal{T}\right)^{-1}\circ\bm{\Xi}=\sum_{t=0}^{\infty}\mathcal{T}^{t}\circ\bm{\Xi}.

Recursion for
\mathcal{T}^{t}\circ\bm{\Xi}
will be as follows,

\displaystyle\mathcal{T}^{t}\circ\bm{\Xi}

\displaystyle=\widetilde{\mathcal{T}}\circ\mathcal{T}^{t-1}\circ\bm{\Xi}+{\mathcal{M}}\circ\mathcal{T}^{t-1}\circ\bm{\Xi},

\displaystyle=\widetilde{\mathcal{T}}^{2}\circ\mathcal{T}^{t-2}\circ\bm{\Xi}+\widetilde{\mathcal{T}}\circ{\mathcal{M}}\circ\mathcal{T}^{t-2}\circ\bm{\Xi}+{\mathcal{M}}\circ\mathcal{T}^{t-1}\circ\bm{\Xi},

\displaystyle=\widetilde{\mathcal{T}}^{t}\circ\bm{\Xi}+\sum_{k=0}^{t-1}\widetilde{\mathcal{T}}^{t-k-1}\circ{\mathcal{M}}\circ\mathcal{T}^{k}\circ\bm{\Xi}.

Taking the sum of these terms from
0
to
\infty

\displaystyle\sum_{t=0}^{\infty}\mathcal{T}^{t}\circ\bm{\Xi}

\displaystyle=\sum_{t=0}^{\infty}\widetilde{\mathcal{T}}^{t}\circ\bm{\Xi}+\sum_{t=0}^{\infty}\sum_{k=0}^{t-1}\widetilde{\mathcal{T}}^{t-k-1}\circ{\mathcal{M}}\circ\mathcal{T}^{k}\circ\bm{\Xi}.

Interchanging the summations in the second part,

\displaystyle\sum_{t=0}^{\infty}\mathcal{T}^{t}\circ\bm{\Xi}

\displaystyle=\left(\sum_{t=0}^{\infty}\widetilde{\mathcal{T}}^{t}\right)\circ\bm{\Xi}+\sum_{k=0}^{\infty}\left(\sum_{t=k+1}^{\infty}\widetilde{\mathcal{T}}^{t-k-1}\right)\circ{\mathcal{M}}\circ\mathcal{T}^{k}\circ\bm{\Xi}

\displaystyle\text{Using }\sum_{t=k+1}^{\infty}\widetilde{\mathcal{T}}^{t-k-1}=\sum_{t=0}^{\infty}\widetilde{\mathcal{T}}^{t}

\displaystyle=(\mathcal{I}-\widetilde{\mathcal{T}})^{-1},

\displaystyle\sum_{t=0}^{\infty}\mathcal{T}^{t}\circ\bm{\Xi}

\displaystyle=\left(\mathcal{I}-\widetilde{\mathcal{T}}\right)^{-1}\circ\bm{\Xi}+\sum_{k=0}^{\infty}\left(\mathcal{I}-\widetilde{\mathcal{T}}\right)^{-1}\circ{\mathcal{M}}\circ\mathcal{T}^{k}\circ\bm{\Xi},

\displaystyle=\left(\mathcal{I}-\widetilde{\mathcal{T}}\right)^{-1}\circ\bm{\Xi}+\left(\mathcal{I}-\widetilde{\mathcal{T}}\right)^{-1}\circ{\mathcal{M}}\circ\sum_{k=0}^{\infty}\mathcal{T}^{k}\circ\bm{\Xi},

\displaystyle\bm{\Phi}_{\scriptscriptstyle\infty}

\displaystyle=\left(\mathcal{I}-\widetilde{\mathcal{T}}\right)^{-1}\circ\bm{\Xi}+\left(\mathcal{I}-\widetilde{\mathcal{T}}\right)^{-1}\circ{\mathcal{M}}\circ\bm{\Phi}_{\scriptscriptstyle\infty}.

From this we have,

\displaystyle\bm{\Phi}_{\scriptscriptstyle\infty}-\left(\mathcal{I}-\widetilde{\mathcal{T}}\right)^{-1}\circ{\mathcal{M}}\circ\bm{\Phi}_{\scriptscriptstyle\infty}

\displaystyle=\left(\mathcal{I}-\widetilde{\mathcal{T}}\right)^{-1}\circ\bm{\Xi},

\displaystyle\left(\mathcal{I}-\left(\mathcal{I}-\widetilde{\mathcal{T}}\right)^{-1}\circ{\mathcal{M}}\right)\circ\bm{\Phi}_{\scriptscriptstyle\infty}

\displaystyle=\left(\mathcal{I}-\widetilde{\mathcal{T}}\right)^{-1}\circ\bm{\Xi},

\displaystyle\bm{\Phi}_{\scriptscriptstyle\infty}

\displaystyle=\left(\mathcal{I}-\left(\mathcal{I}-\widetilde{\mathcal{T}}\right)^{-1}\circ{\mathcal{M}}\right)^{-1}\circ\left(\mathcal{I}-\widetilde{\mathcal{T}}\right)^{-1}\circ\bm{\Xi}.

Writing the inverse as a sum of exponential series gives us

\displaystyle\bm{\Phi}_{\scriptscriptstyle\infty}

\displaystyle=\sum_{t=0}^{\infty}\left(\left(\mathcal{I}-\widetilde{\mathcal{T}}\right)^{-1}\circ{\mathcal{M}}\right)^{t}\circ\left(\mathcal{I}-\widetilde{\mathcal{T}}\right)^{-1}\circ\bm{\Xi},

(55)

Note
(\alpha+2\beta)R^{2}\leq 1\implies(\alpha+2\beta)\lambda_{\max}\leq 1
. Hence we can invoke Lemma
33
here.

\displaystyle{\mathcal{M}}\circ(1-\widetilde{\mathcal{T}})^{-1}\circ\bm{\Xi}

\displaystyle\preccurlyeq\left[\frac{2\alpha\tilde{\kappa}}{3\beta}+\frac{(\alpha+2\beta)R^{2}}{3}\right]\bm{\Xi}

Using

(\alpha+2\beta)R^{2}\leq 1,\alpha\leq\frac{\beta}{2\tilde{\kappa}},

\displaystyle{\mathcal{M}}\circ(1-\widetilde{\mathcal{T}})^{-1}\circ\bm{\Xi}

\displaystyle\preccurlyeq\left[\frac{1}{3}+\frac{1}{3}\right]\bm{\Xi}\preccurlyeq\frac{2}{3}\bm{\Xi}.

Using this in Eq.(
55
),

\displaystyle\bm{\Phi}_{\scriptscriptstyle\infty}

\displaystyle=\sum_{t=0}^{\infty}\left(\left(\mathcal{I}-\widetilde{\mathcal{T}}\right)^{-1}\circ{\mathcal{M}}\right)^{t}\circ\left(\mathcal{I}-\widetilde{\mathcal{T}}\right)^{-1}\circ\bm{\Xi},

\displaystyle\text{Use }\left(\left(\mathcal{I}-\widetilde{\mathcal{T}}\right)^{-1}\circ{\mathcal{M}}\right)^{t}

\displaystyle\circ\left(\mathcal{I}-\widetilde{\mathcal{T}}\right)^{-1}=\left(\mathcal{I}-\widetilde{\mathcal{T}}\right)^{-1}\circ\left({\mathcal{M}}\circ\left(\mathcal{I}-\widetilde{\mathcal{T}}\right)^{-1}\right)^{t},

\displaystyle\bm{\Phi}_{\scriptscriptstyle\infty}

\displaystyle=\left(\mathcal{I}-\widetilde{\mathcal{T}}\right)^{-1}\circ\sum_{t=0}^{\infty}\left({\mathcal{M}}\circ\left(\mathcal{I}-\widetilde{\mathcal{T}}\right)^{-1}\right)^{t}\circ\bm{\Xi},

\displaystyle\text{Using }\left({\mathcal{M}}\circ\left(\mathcal{I}-\widetilde{\mathcal{T}}\right)^{-1}\right)^{t}

\displaystyle\preccurlyeq\left[\frac{2}{3}\right]^{t}\bm{\Xi},

\displaystyle\bm{\Phi}_{\scriptscriptstyle\infty}

\displaystyle\preccurlyeq\left(\mathcal{I}-\widetilde{\mathcal{T}}\right)^{-1}\circ\sum_{t=0}^{\infty}\left[\frac{2}{3}\right]^{t}\cdot\bm{\Xi},

\displaystyle\preccurlyeq\left(\mathcal{I}-\widetilde{\mathcal{T}}\right)^{-1}\circ 3\cdot\bm{\Xi}.

This completes the proof.

Lemma 31

For any block matrix
\begin{bmatrix}P&Q\\
R&S\end{bmatrix}
,

\displaystyle{\mathcal{M}}\circ\begin{bmatrix}P&Q\\
R&S\end{bmatrix}

\displaystyle=\begin{bmatrix}\beta^{2}&\alpha\beta\\
\alpha\beta&\alpha^{2}\end{bmatrix}\otimes_{k}{\mathbb{E}}\left[\left(\mathbf{H}-aa^{\top}\right)(P+Q+R+S)\left(\mathbf{H}-aa^{\top}\right)\right]

Proof

\displaystyle{\mathcal{M}}\circ\begin{bmatrix}P&Q\\
R&S\end{bmatrix}

\displaystyle={\mathbb{E}}\left[\begin{bmatrix}\beta\mathbf{H}_{a}&\beta\mathbf{H}_{a}\\
\alpha\mathbf{H}_{a}&\alpha\mathbf{H}_{a}\end{bmatrix}\begin{bmatrix}P&Q\\
R&S\end{bmatrix}\begin{bmatrix}\beta\mathbf{H}_{a}&\alpha\mathbf{H}_{a}\\
\beta\mathbf{H}_{a}&\alpha\mathbf{H}_{a}\end{bmatrix}\right]

where
\mathbf{H}_{a}=\left(\mathbf{H}-aa^{\top}\right)

\displaystyle\begin{bmatrix}\beta\mathbf{H}_{a}&\beta\mathbf{H}_{a}\\
\alpha\mathbf{H}_{a}&\alpha\mathbf{H}_{a}\end{bmatrix}\begin{bmatrix}P&Q\\
R&S\end{bmatrix}

\displaystyle=\begin{bmatrix}\beta\mathbf{H}_{a}(P+R)&\beta\mathbf{H}_{a}(Q+S)\\
\alpha\mathbf{H}_{a}(P+R)&\alpha\mathbf{H}_{a}(Q+S)\end{bmatrix},

\displaystyle\begin{bmatrix}\beta\mathbf{H}_{a}(P+R)&\beta\mathbf{H}_{a}(Q+S)\\
\alpha\mathbf{H}_{a}(P+R)&\alpha\mathbf{H}_{a}(Q+S)\end{bmatrix}\begin{bmatrix}\beta\mathbf{H}_{a}&\alpha\mathbf{H}_{a}\\
\beta\mathbf{H}_{a}&\alpha\mathbf{H}_{a}\end{bmatrix}

\displaystyle=\begin{bmatrix}\beta^{2}\mathbf{H}_{a}(P+Q+R+S)\mathbf{H}_{a}&\alpha\beta\mathbf{H}_{a}(P+Q+R+S)\mathbf{H}_{a}\\
\alpha\beta\mathbf{H}_{a}(P+Q+R+S)\mathbf{H}_{a}&\alpha^{2}\mathbf{H}_{a}(P+Q+R+S)\mathbf{H}_{a}\end{bmatrix},

\displaystyle=\begin{bmatrix}\beta^{2}&\alpha\beta\\
\alpha\beta&\alpha^{2}\end{bmatrix}\otimes_{k}\mathbf{H}_{a}(P+Q+R+S)\mathbf{H}_{a}.

Taking expectation,

\displaystyle{\mathcal{M}}\circ\begin{bmatrix}P&Q\\
R&S\end{bmatrix}

\displaystyle=\begin{bmatrix}\beta^{2}&\alpha\beta\\
\alpha\beta&\alpha^{2}\end{bmatrix}\otimes_{k}{\mathbb{E}}\left[\mathbf{H}_{a}(P+Q+R+S)\mathbf{H}_{a}\right].

This completes the proof.

Lemma 32

Under Assumption
4
, for any
t\geq 0
,

\displaystyle{\mathcal{M}}\circ{\mathbb{E}}\left[\bm{\theta}_{t}\otimes\bm{\theta}_{t}\right]\preccurlyeq\kappa\left\langle{\Upsilon},{{\mathbb{E}}\left[\bm{\theta}_{t}\otimes\bm{\theta}_{t}\right]}\right\rangle\bm{\Xi}.

Proof

\displaystyle{\mathcal{M}}\circ{\mathbb{E}}\left[\bm{\theta}_{t}\otimes\bm{\theta}_{t}\right]

\displaystyle={\mathbb{E}}\left[\mathcal{J}{\mathbb{E}}\left[\bm{\theta}_{t}\otimes\bm{\theta}_{t}\right]\mathcal{J}^{\top}\right],

\displaystyle\bm{\theta}_{t}\otimes\bm{\theta}_{t}

\displaystyle=\begin{bmatrix}\bm{v}_{t}\\
\bm{w}_{t}\\
\end{bmatrix}\otimes\begin{bmatrix}\bm{v}_{t}\\
\bm{w}_{t}\\
\end{bmatrix}=\begin{bmatrix}\bm{v}_{t}\bm{v}_{t}^{\top}&\bm{v}_{t}\bm{w}_{t}^{\top}\\
\bm{w}_{t}\bm{v}_{t}^{\top}&\bm{w}_{t}\bm{w}_{t}^{\top}\end{bmatrix},

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}\left[\bm{\theta}_{t}\otimes\bm{\theta}_{t}\right]

\displaystyle=\begin{bmatrix}{\mathbb{E}}\left[\bm{v}_{t}\bm{v}_{t}^{\top}\right]&{\mathbb{E}}\left[\bm{v}_{t}\bm{w}_{t}^{\top}\right]\\
{\mathbb{E}}\left[\bm{w}_{t}\bm{v}_{t}^{\top}\right]&{\mathbb{E}}\left[\bm{w}_{t}\bm{w}_{t}^{\top}\right]\end{bmatrix}.

As
J
and
{\mathbb{E}}\left[\bm{\theta}_{t}\otimes\bm{\theta}_{t}\right]
are independent, invoking Lemma
31
with

\displaystyle\begin{bmatrix}P&Q\\
R&S\end{bmatrix}

\displaystyle=\begin{bmatrix}{\mathbb{E}}\left[\bm{v}_{t}\bm{v}_{t}^{\top}\right]&{\mathbb{E}}\left[\bm{v}_{t}\bm{w}_{t}^{\top}\right]\\
{\mathbb{E}}\left[\bm{w}_{t}\bm{v}_{t}^{\top}\right]&{\mathbb{E}}\left[\bm{w}_{t}\bm{w}_{t}^{\top}\right]\end{bmatrix}.

Now
P+Q+R+S
in our case will be

\displaystyle P+Q+R+S

\displaystyle={\mathbb{E}}\left[\bm{v}_{t}\bm{v}_{t}^{\top}+\bm{v}_{t}\bm{w}_{t}^{\top}+\bm{w}_{t}\bm{v}_{t}^{\top}+\bm{w}_{t}\bm{w}_{t}^{\top}\right],

\displaystyle={\mathbb{E}}\left[\left(\bm{v}_{t}+\bm{w}_{t}\right)\left(\bm{v}_{t}+\bm{w}_{t}\right)^{\top}\right]={\mathbb{E}}\left[\bm{u}_{t}\bm{u}_{t}^{\top}\right],\text{ from {Eq}.(\ref{eq:xt}) . }

Using this we get

\displaystyle{\mathcal{M}}\circ{\mathbb{E}}\left[\bm{\theta}_{t}\otimes\bm{\theta}_{t}\right]=\begin{bmatrix}\beta^{2}&\alpha\beta\\
\alpha\beta&\alpha^{2}\end{bmatrix}\otimes_{k}{\mathbb{E}}\left[\left(\mathbf{H}-aa^{\top}\right){\mathbb{E}}\left[\bm{u}_{t}\bm{u}_{t}^{\top}\right]\left(\mathbf{H}-aa^{\top}\right)\right].

Using Property
1
,

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}\left[\left(\mathbf{H}-aa^{\top}\right){\mathbb{E}}\left[\bm{u}_{t}\bm{u}_{t}^{\top}\right]\left(\mathbf{H}-aa^{\top}\right)\right]\preccurlyeq{\mathbb{E}}\left[aa^{\top}{\mathbb{E}}\left[\bm{u}_{t}\bm{u}_{t}^{\top}\right]aa^{\top}\right]={\mathbb{E}}\left[\left\langle{a},{{\mathbb{E}}\left[\bm{u}_{t}\bm{u}_{t}^{\top}\right]a}\right\rangle aa^{\top}\right].

Using Assumption
4
with
M={\mathbb{E}}\left[\bm{u}_{t}\bm{u}_{t}^{\top}\right]
,

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}\left[\left\langle{a},{{\mathbb{E}}\left[\bm{u}_{t}\bm{u}_{t}^{\top}\right]a}\right\rangle aa^{\top}\right]

\displaystyle\preccurlyeq\kappa\mathrm{Tr}{\left({\mathbb{E}}\left[\mathbf{H}\bm{u}_{t}\bm{u}_{t}^{\top}\right]\right)}\mathbf{H}.

As kronecker product of two PSD matrices is positive,

\displaystyle{\mathcal{M}}\circ{\mathbb{E}}\left[\bm{\theta}_{t}\otimes\bm{\theta}_{t}\right]

\displaystyle\preccurlyeq\begin{bmatrix}\beta^{2}&\alpha\beta\\
\alpha\beta&\alpha^{2}\end{bmatrix}\otimes_{k}\kappa\mathrm{Tr}{\left({\mathbb{E}}\left[\mathbf{H}\bm{u}_{t}\bm{u}_{t}^{\top}\right]\right)}\mathbf{H},

\displaystyle=\kappa\mathrm{Tr}{\left({\mathbb{E}}\left[\mathbf{H}\bm{u}_{t}\bm{u}_{t}^{\top}\right]\right)}\begin{bmatrix}\beta^{2}\mathbf{H}&\alpha\beta\mathbf{H}\\
\alpha\beta\mathbf{H}&\alpha^{2}\mathbf{H}\end{bmatrix}=\kappa\mathrm{Tr}{\left({\mathbb{E}}\left[\mathbf{H}\bm{u}_{t}\bm{u}_{t}^{\top}\right]\right)}\bm{\Xi}.

Noting that
\left\langle{\Upsilon},{{\mathbb{E}}\left[\bm{\theta}_{t}\otimes\bm{\theta}_{t}\right]}\right\rangle=\mathrm{Tr}{\left({\mathbb{E}}\left[\mathbf{H}\bm{u}_{t}\bm{u}_{t}^{\top}\right]\right)}
completes the proof.

Lemma 33

With

(\alpha+2\beta)R^{2}\leq 1,\alpha\leq\frac{\beta}{2\tilde{\kappa}}
,

\displaystyle{\mathcal{M}}\circ(1-\widetilde{\mathcal{T}})^{-1}\circ\bm{\Xi}

\displaystyle\preccurlyeq\frac{2}{3}\bm{\Xi}

Proof

Note
(\alpha+2\beta)R^{2}\leq 1\implies(\alpha+2\beta)\lambda_{\max}\leq 1
. Hence we can invoke Lemma
29
here.

\displaystyle{\mathcal{M}}\circ(1-\widetilde{\mathcal{T}})^{-1}\circ\bm{\Xi}\preccurlyeq{\mathcal{M}}\circ\frac{1}{3}\begin{bmatrix}2\alpha(\beta\mathbf{H})^{-1}+(2\beta-3\alpha)\mathbf{I}&\alpha\beta^{-1}(2\beta-\alpha)\mathbf{I}\\
\alpha\beta^{-1}(2\beta-\alpha)\mathbf{I}&2\alpha^{2}\beta^{-1}\mathbf{I}\end{bmatrix}.

Invoking the Lemma
31
for a block matrix,

\displaystyle\begin{bmatrix}P&Q\\
R&S\end{bmatrix}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{3}\begin{bmatrix}2\alpha(\beta\mathbf{H})^{-1}+(2\beta-3\alpha)\mathbf{I}&\alpha\beta^{-1}(2\beta-\alpha)\mathbf{I}\\
\alpha\beta^{-1}(2\beta-\alpha)\mathbf{I}&2\alpha^{2}\beta^{-1}\mathbf{I}\end{bmatrix}.

Now
P+Q+R+S
in our case is

\displaystyle 3*(P+Q+R+S)

\displaystyle=2\alpha(\beta\mathbf{H})^{-1}+(2\beta-3\alpha)\mathbf{I}+2\alpha\beta^{-1}(2\beta-\alpha)\mathbf{I}+2\alpha^{2}\beta^{-1}\mathbf{I},

\displaystyle=2\alpha(\beta\mathbf{H})^{-1}+(2\beta-3\alpha)\mathbf{I}+4\alpha\mathbf{I}-2\alpha^{2}\beta^{-1}\mathbf{I}++2\alpha^{2}\beta^{-1}\mathbf{I},

\displaystyle=2\alpha(\beta\mathbf{H})^{-1}+(\alpha+2\beta)\mathbf{I}.

\displaystyle{\mathcal{M}}\circ(1-\widetilde{\mathcal{T}})^{-1}\circ\bm{\Xi}=\frac{1}{3}\begin{bmatrix}\beta^{2}&\alpha\beta\\
\alpha\beta&\alpha^{2}\end{bmatrix}\otimes_{k}{\mathbb{E}}\left[\left(\mathbf{H}-aa^{\top}\right)\left[2\alpha(\beta\mathbf{H})^{-1}+(\alpha+2\beta)\mathbf{I}\right]\left(\mathbf{H}-aa^{\top}\right)\right].

(56)

Using Property
1
,

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}\left[\left(\mathbf{H}-aa^{\top}\right)\left[2\alpha(\beta\mathbf{H})^{-1}+(\alpha+2\beta)\mathbf{I}\right]\left(\mathbf{H}-aa^{\top}\right)\right]

\displaystyle\preccurlyeq{\mathbb{E}}\left[aa^{\top}\left[2\alpha(\beta\mathbf{H})^{-1}+(\alpha+2\beta)\mathbf{I}\right]aa^{\top}\right],

\displaystyle=2\frac{\alpha}{\beta}{\mathbb{E}}\left[\bigl\|{a}\bigr\|_{\mathbf{H}^{-1}}^{2}~aa^{\top}\right]+(\alpha+2\beta){\mathbb{E}}\left[\bigl\|{a}\bigr\|^{2}~aa^{\top}\right].

Using the Assumptions
1
,
3
of the feature distribution, we have

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}\left[\left(\mathbf{H}-aa^{\top}\right)\left[2\alpha(\beta\mathbf{H})^{-1}+(\alpha+2\beta)\mathbf{I}\right]\left(\mathbf{H}-aa^{\top}\right)\right]

\displaystyle\preccurlyeq\frac{2\alpha\tilde{\kappa}}{\beta}\mathbf{H}+(\alpha+2\beta)R^{2}\mathbf{H},

Using the above in Eq.(
56
) and that fact that kronecker product of two PSD matrices is positive we get,

\displaystyle{\mathcal{M}}\circ(1-\widetilde{\mathcal{T}})^{-1}\circ\bm{\Xi}

\displaystyle\preccurlyeq\frac{1}{3}\begin{bmatrix}\beta^{2}&\alpha\beta\\
\alpha\beta&\alpha^{2}\end{bmatrix}\otimes_{k}\left[\frac{2\alpha\tilde{\kappa}}{\beta}\mathbf{H}+(\alpha+2\beta)R^{2}\mathbf{H}\right],

\displaystyle=\left[\frac{2\alpha\tilde{\kappa}}{3\beta}+\frac{(\alpha+2\beta)R^{2}}{3}\right]\left(\begin{bmatrix}\beta^{2}&\alpha\beta\\
\alpha\beta&\alpha^{2}\end{bmatrix}\otimes_{k}\mathbf{H}\right),

\displaystyle=\left[\frac{2\alpha\tilde{\kappa}}{3\beta}+\frac{(\alpha+2\beta)R^{2}}{3}\right]\bm{\Xi}.

where the last step is from the definition of
\bm{\Xi}
.
Using

(\alpha+2\beta)R^{2}\leq 1,\alpha\leq\frac{\beta}{2\tilde{\kappa}},

\displaystyle{\mathcal{M}}\circ(1-\widetilde{\mathcal{T}})^{-1}\circ\bm{\Xi}

\displaystyle\preccurlyeq\left[\frac{1}{3}+\frac{1}{3}\right]\bm{\Xi}\preccurlyeq\frac{2}{3}\bm{\Xi}.

Lemma 34

With
(\alpha+2\beta)R^{2}\leq 1,\alpha\leq\frac{\beta}{2\tilde{\kappa}},
and
\Upsilon
from Eq.(
31
), we have,

\displaystyle{\mathcal{M}}^{\top}\circ\left(\mathcal{I}-\widetilde{\mathcal{T}}^{\top}\right)^{-1}\circ\Upsilon

\displaystyle\preccurlyeq\frac{2}{3}\Upsilon.

(57)

Proof

Compute the inverse by evaluating the summation of the following infinite series,

\displaystyle\left(\mathcal{I}-\widetilde{\mathcal{T}}^{\top}\right)^{-1}\circ\Upsilon=\sum_{t=0}^{\infty}\left(\widetilde{\mathcal{T}}^{t}\right)^{\top}\circ\bm{\Xi}

\displaystyle=\sum_{t=0}^{\infty}(\mathcal{A}^{t})^{\top}\cdot\Upsilon\cdot\mathcal{A}^{t}.

From this it follows that,

\displaystyle{\mathcal{M}}^{\top}\circ\left(\mathcal{I}-\widetilde{\mathcal{T}}^{\top}\right)^{-1}={\mathbb{E}}\left[\mathcal{J}^{\top}\cdot\left((\mathcal{A}^{t})^{\top}\cdot\Upsilon\cdot\mathcal{A}^{t}\right)\cdot\mathcal{J}\right].

•

Both
\mathcal{A}
and
\Upsilon
are diagonizable wrt to the eigen basis of
\mathbf{H}
. We will now project these block matrices onto their eigen basis and compute the summation of each component individually. Note that,

\displaystyle\mathcal{A}=\sum_{i}\mathcal{A}_{i}\otimes_{k}e_{i}e_{i}^{\top},\qquad\Upsilon=\sum_{i}\Upsilon_{i}\otimes_{k}e_{i}e_{i}^{\top}.

(58)

where
\mathcal{A}_{i}
and
\Upsilon_{i}
are

\displaystyle\mathcal{A}_{i}=\begin{bmatrix}1-\beta\lambda_{i}&1-\beta\lambda_{i}\\
-\alpha\lambda_{i}&1-\alpha\lambda_{i}\end{bmatrix}\qquad\Upsilon_{i}=\begin{bmatrix}\lambda_{i}&\lambda_{i}\\
\lambda_{i}&\lambda_{i}\end{bmatrix}.

Using these projections,

\displaystyle(\mathcal{A}^{t})^{\top}\cdot\Upsilon\cdot(\mathcal{A}^{t})

\displaystyle=\sum_{i}\left((\mathcal{A}_{i}^{t})^{\top}\cdot\Upsilon_{i}\cdot\mathcal{A}_{i}^{t}\right)\otimes_{k}e_{i}e_{i}^{\top}.

•

Now the random matrix

\displaystyle\mathcal{J}=\begin{bmatrix}\beta\left(H-aa^{\top}\right)&\beta\left(H-aa^{\top}\right)\\
\alpha\left(H-aa^{\top}\right)&\alpha\left(H-aa^{\top}\right)\end{bmatrix}=\begin{bmatrix}\beta&\beta\\
\alpha&\alpha\end{bmatrix}\otimes_{k}\left(H-aa^{\top}\right).

From the mixed product property of kronecker product i.e for any matrices of appropriate dimension
P,Q,R,S

\left(P\otimes_{k}Q\right)\left(R\otimes_{k}S\right)=PR\otimes_{k}QS.

\displaystyle\begin{aligned} {\mathbb{E}}\left[\mathcal{J}^{\top}\cdot\left[\left((\mathcal{A}_{i}^{t})^{\top}\cdot\Upsilon_{i}\cdot\mathcal{A}_{i}^{t}\right)\otimes_{k}e_{i}e_{i}^{\top}\right]\cdot\mathcal{J}\right]&=\begin{bmatrix}\beta&\alpha\\
\beta&\alpha\end{bmatrix}\left((\mathcal{A}_{i}^{t})^{\top}\cdot\Upsilon_{i}\cdot\mathcal{A}_{i}^{t}\right)\begin{bmatrix}\beta&\beta\\
\alpha&\alpha\end{bmatrix}\\
&\hskip 71.13188pt\otimes_{k}{\mathbb{E}}\left[\left(H-aa^{\top}\right)e_{i}e_{i}^{\top}\left(H-aa^{\top}\right)\right].\end{aligned}

(59)

Using the above observations,

\displaystyle\sum_{t=0}^{\infty}(\mathcal{A}^{t})^{\top}\cdot\Upsilon\cdot(\mathcal{A}^{t})

\displaystyle=\sum_{t=0}^{\infty}\sum_{i}\left(\left(\mathcal{A}_{i}^{t}\right)^{\top}\cdot\Upsilon_{i}\cdot\left(\mathcal{A}_{i}^{t}\right)\right)\otimes_{k}e_{i}e_{i}^{\top},

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}\left[\sum_{t=0}^{\infty}\mathcal{J}^{\top}(\mathcal{A}^{t})^{\top}\cdot\Upsilon\cdot(\mathcal{A}^{t})\mathcal{J}\right]

\displaystyle=\sum_{t=0}^{\infty}\sum_{i}{\mathbb{E}}\left[\mathcal{J}^{\top}\cdot\left[\left((\mathcal{A}_{i}^{t})^{\top}\cdot\Upsilon_{i}\cdot\mathcal{A}_{i}^{t}\right)\otimes_{k}e_{i}e_{i}^{\top}\right]\cdot\mathcal{J}\right],

\displaystyle=\sum_{i}\sum_{t=0}^{\infty}\begin{bmatrix}\beta&\alpha\\
\beta&\alpha\end{bmatrix}\left((\mathcal{A}_{i}^{t})^{\top}\cdot\Upsilon_{i}\cdot\mathcal{A}_{i}^{t}\right)\begin{bmatrix}\beta&\beta\\
\alpha&\alpha\end{bmatrix}\otimes_{k}{\mathbb{E}}\left[\left(H-aa^{\top}\right)e_{i}e_{i}^{\top}\left(H-aa^{\top}\right)\right].

Hence,

\displaystyle{\mathcal{M}}^{\top}\circ\left(\mathcal{I}-\widetilde{\mathcal{T}}^{\top}\right)^{-1}\circ\Upsilon

\displaystyle=\sum_{i}\left(\sum_{t=0}^{\infty}\begin{bmatrix}\beta&\alpha\\
\beta&\alpha\end{bmatrix}\left((\mathcal{A}_{i}^{t})^{\top}\cdot\Upsilon_{i}\cdot\mathcal{A}_{i}^{t}\right)\begin{bmatrix}\beta&\beta\\
\alpha&\alpha\end{bmatrix}\right)\otimes_{k}{\mathbb{E}}\left[\left(H-aa^{\top}\right)e_{i}e_{i}^{\top}\left(H-aa^{\top}\right)\right].

(60)

From the definition of
\Upsilon_{i}
,

\displaystyle\lambda_{i}\left(\sum_{t=0}^{\infty}\begin{bmatrix}\beta&\alpha\\
\beta&\alpha\end{bmatrix}\left((\mathcal{A}_{i}^{t})^{\top}\cdot\Upsilon_{i}\cdot\mathcal{A}_{i}^{t}\right)\begin{bmatrix}\beta&\alpha\\
\beta&\alpha\end{bmatrix}\right)

\displaystyle=\sum_{t=0}^{\infty}\begin{bmatrix}\beta\lambda_{i}&\alpha\lambda_{i}\\
\beta\lambda_{i}&\alpha\lambda_{i}\end{bmatrix}\left((\mathcal{A}_{i}^{t})^{\top}\cdot\begin{bmatrix}1&1\\
1&1\end{bmatrix}\cdot\mathcal{A}_{i}^{t}\right)\begin{bmatrix}\beta\lambda_{i}&\beta\lambda_{i}\\
\alpha\lambda_{i}&\alpha\lambda_{i}\end{bmatrix}.

With
b=\beta\lambda_{i},a=\alpha\lambda_{i}
,

\displaystyle\mathcal{A}_{i}=\begin{bmatrix}1-b&1-b\\
-a&1-a\end{bmatrix}.

Hence to compute this series we can invoke Lemma
38
with
\Gamma=\mathcal{A}_{i}
,

\displaystyle\sum_{t=0}^{\infty}\begin{bmatrix}\beta\lambda_{i}&\alpha\lambda_{i}\\
\beta\lambda_{i}&\alpha\lambda_{i}\end{bmatrix}\left((\mathcal{A}_{i}^{t})^{\top}\cdot\begin{bmatrix}1&1\\
1&1\end{bmatrix}\cdot\mathcal{A}_{i}^{t}\right)\begin{bmatrix}\beta\lambda_{i}&\beta\lambda_{i}\\
\alpha\lambda_{i}&\alpha\lambda_{i}\end{bmatrix}

\displaystyle=\left(\frac{2a}{b(4-(a+2b))}+\frac{a+2b}{(4-(a+2b))}\right)\begin{bmatrix}1&1\\
1&1\end{bmatrix},

\displaystyle\lambda_{i}\left(\sum_{t=0}^{\infty}\begin{bmatrix}\beta&\alpha\\
\beta&\alpha\end{bmatrix}\left((\mathcal{A}_{i}^{t})^{\top}\cdot\Upsilon_{i}\cdot\mathcal{A}_{i}^{t}\right)\begin{bmatrix}\beta&\alpha\\
\beta&\alpha\end{bmatrix}\right)

\displaystyle=\left(\frac{2\alpha\lambda_{i}}{\beta\lambda_{i}(4-(\alpha\lambda_{i}+2\beta\lambda_{i}))}+\frac{\alpha\lambda_{i}+2\beta\lambda_{i}}{(4-(\alpha\lambda_{i}+2\beta\lambda_{i}))}\right)\begin{bmatrix}1&1\\
1&1\end{bmatrix},
