\displaystyle\left(\sum_{t=0}^{\infty}\begin{bmatrix}\beta&\alpha\\
\beta&\alpha\end{bmatrix}\left((\mathcal{A}_{i}^{t})^{\top}\cdot\Upsilon_{i}\cdot\mathcal{A}_{i}^{t}\right)\begin{bmatrix}\beta&\alpha\\
\beta&\alpha\end{bmatrix}\right)

\displaystyle=\left(\frac{2\alpha}{\beta\lambda_{i}(4-(\alpha\lambda_{i}+2\beta\lambda_{i}))}+\frac{\alpha+2\beta}{(4-(\alpha\lambda_{i}+2\beta\lambda_{i}))}\right)\begin{bmatrix}1&1\\
1&1\end{bmatrix}.

We have

\displaystyle(\alpha+2\beta)\lambda_{i}\leq(\alpha+2\beta)\lambda_{\max}\leq(\alpha+2\beta)R^{2}\leq 1,

\displaystyle\text{Hence, }4-((\alpha+2\beta)\lambda_{i})\geq 3,

\displaystyle\frac{1}{4-((\alpha+2\beta)\lambda_{i})}\leq\frac{1}{3},

\displaystyle\frac{2\alpha}{\beta\lambda_{i}(4-(\alpha\lambda_{i}+2\beta\lambda_{i}))}+\frac{\alpha+2\beta}{(4-(\alpha\lambda_{i}+2\beta\lambda_{i}))}\leq\frac{1}{3}\left(\frac{2\alpha}{\beta\lambda_{i}}+(\alpha+2\beta)\right).

As the matrix
\begin{bmatrix}1&1\\
1&1\end{bmatrix}
is PSD,

\displaystyle\left(\sum_{t=0}^{\infty}\begin{bmatrix}\beta&\alpha\\
\beta&\alpha\end{bmatrix}\left((\mathcal{A}_{i}^{t})^{\top}\cdot\Upsilon_{i}\cdot\mathcal{A}_{i}^{t}\right)\begin{bmatrix}\beta&\alpha\\
\beta&\alpha\end{bmatrix}\right)

\displaystyle\preccurlyeq\frac{1}{3}\left(\frac{2\alpha}{\beta\lambda_{i}}+(\alpha+2\beta)\right)\begin{bmatrix}1&1\\
1&1\end{bmatrix}.

Using the Property
1
,

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}\left[\left(H-aa^{\top}\right)e_{i}e_{i}^{\top}\left(H-aa^{\top}\right)\right]\preccurlyeq{\mathbb{E}}\left[aa^{\top}\cdot e_{i}e_{i}^{\top}\cdot aa^{\top}\right].

Using the above two results and the fact that for any PSD matrices
P,Q,R,S
,
P\preccurlyeq Q
and
R\preccurlyeq S
then
P\otimes_{k}R\preccurlyeq Q\otimes_{k}S
. Hence from Eq.(
60
) we can get the bound as follows

\displaystyle{\mathcal{M}}^{\top}\circ\left(\mathcal{I}-\widetilde{\mathcal{T}}^{\top}\right)^{-1}\circ\Upsilon

\displaystyle=\sum_{i}\left(\sum_{t=0}^{\infty}\begin{bmatrix}\beta&\alpha\\
\beta&\alpha\end{bmatrix}\left((\mathcal{A}_{i}^{t})^{\top}\cdot\Upsilon_{i}\cdot\mathcal{A}_{i}^{t}\right)\begin{bmatrix}\beta&\beta\\
\alpha&\alpha\end{bmatrix}\right)\otimes_{k}{\mathbb{E}}\left[\left(H-aa^{\top}\right)e_{i}e_{i}^{\top}\left(H-aa^{\top}\right)\right],

\displaystyle\preccurlyeq\sum_{i}\frac{1}{3}\left(\frac{2\alpha}{\beta\lambda_{i}}+(\alpha+2\beta)\right)\begin{bmatrix}1&1\\
1&1\end{bmatrix}\otimes_{k}{\mathbb{E}}\left[aa^{\top}\cdot e_{i}e_{i}^{\top}\cdot aa^{\top}\right],

\displaystyle=\begin{bmatrix}1&1\\
1&1\end{bmatrix}\otimes_{k}{\mathbb{E}}\left[aa^{\top}\cdot\sum_{i}\left[\left(\frac{2\alpha}{3\beta\lambda_{i}}+\frac{(\alpha+2\beta)}{3}\right)e_{i}e_{i}^{\top}\right]\cdot aa^{\top}\right],

\displaystyle=\begin{bmatrix}1&1\\
1&1\end{bmatrix}\otimes_{k}{\mathbb{E}}\left[aa^{\top}\cdot\left[\frac{2\alpha}{3\beta}\mathbf{H}^{-1}+\frac{(\alpha+2\beta)}{3}\mathbf{I}\right]\cdot aa^{\top}\right],

\displaystyle=\begin{bmatrix}1&1\\
1&1\end{bmatrix}\otimes_{k}\left[\frac{2\alpha}{3\beta}{\mathbb{E}}\left[\bigl\|{a}\bigr\|^{2}_{\mathbf{H}^{-1}}aa^{\top}\right]+\frac{(\alpha+2\beta)}{3}{\mathbb{E}}\left[\bigl\|{a}\bigr\|^{2}aa^{\top}\right]\right].

Using the Assumptions
1
,
3
of the feature distribution, we have

\displaystyle\frac{2\alpha}{3\beta}{\mathbb{E}}\left[\bigl\|{a}\bigr\|^{2}_{\mathbf{H}^{-1}}aa^{\top}\right]+\frac{(\alpha+2\beta)}{3}{\mathbb{E}}\left[\bigl\|{a}\bigr\|^{2}aa^{\top}\right]\preccurlyeq\left(\frac{2\alpha\tilde{\kappa}}{3\beta}+\frac{(\alpha+2\beta)R^{2}}{3}\right)\mathbf{H}.

Using

(\alpha+2\beta)R^{2}\leq 1,\alpha\leq\frac{\beta}{2\tilde{\kappa}},

\displaystyle\frac{2\alpha}{3\beta}{\mathbb{E}}\left[\bigl\|{a}\bigr\|^{2}_{\mathbf{H}^{-1}}aa^{\top}\right]+\frac{(\alpha+2\beta)}{3}{\mathbb{E}}\left[\bigl\|{a}\bigr\|^{2}aa^{\top}\right]\preccurlyeq\left(\frac{1}{3}+\frac{1}{3}\right)\mathbf{H}=\frac{2}{3}\mathbf{H}.

Using this bound, kronecker product of two PSD matrices is positive completes the proof.

\displaystyle{\mathcal{M}}^{\top}\circ\left(\mathcal{I}-\widetilde{\mathcal{T}}^{\top}\right)^{-1}\circ\Upsilon\preccurlyeq\begin{bmatrix}1&1\\
1&1\end{bmatrix}\otimes_{k}\frac{2}{3}\mathbf{H}=\frac{2}{3}\begin{bmatrix}\mathbf{H}&\mathbf{H}\\
\mathbf{H}&\mathbf{H}\end{bmatrix}.

Appendix E
Technical Lemmas

Property 2
(Eigen Decomposition of
\Gamma
)

For the matrix

\displaystyle\Gamma=\begin{bmatrix}1-b&1-b\\
-a&1-a\end{bmatrix}

(61)

The eigen values of
~\Gamma
are given by

\displaystyle r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{+}}=1-\frac{(a+b)}{2}+\sqrt{\left(\frac{a+b}{2}\right)^{2}-a}\qquad r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{-}}=1-\frac{(a+b)}{2}-\sqrt{\left(\frac{a+b}{2}\right)^{2}-a}

(62)

The eigen decomposition
\Gamma=\mathbf{U}\bm{\Lambda}\mathbf{U}^{-1}
where

\displaystyle\mathbf{U}=\frac{1}{{\scriptstyle\bm{\Delta}}}\begin{bmatrix}\frac{r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{-}}}{(1-r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{-}})}&1\\
1&\frac{1-r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{+}}}{r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{+}}}\end{bmatrix}

\displaystyle\qquad\mathbf{U}^{-1}=\begin{bmatrix}-a&(1-r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{-}})r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{+}}\\
(1-r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{-}})r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{+}}&-r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{+}}r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{-}}\end{bmatrix}

(63)

\displaystyle\bm{\Lambda}

\displaystyle=\begin{bmatrix}r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{+}}&0\\
0&r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{-}}\end{bmatrix}

(64)

\displaystyle{\scriptstyle\bm{\Delta}}

\displaystyle=r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{+}}-r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{-}}

(65)

The following observations hold

•

\mathbf{U}
and
\mathbf{U}^{-1}
are symmetric and
r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{+}},r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{-}}
can be complex as
\Gamma
is not symmetric.

•

For
0<a,b<1
,
|r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{+}}|,|r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{-}}|<1

Lemma 35

For
r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{+}},r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{-}}
given in the Property
2
, the following bound holds

\displaystyle\left[\frac{\left(1-r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{+}}\right)r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{+}}^{t}-\left(1-r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{-}}\right)r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{-}}^{t}}{{\scriptstyle\bm{\Delta}}}\right]^{2}\leq 1+\frac{a}{\left(1-b\right)^{2}}.

(66)

Proof

To prove this conside the one-dimensional nesterov sequences starting form
x_{0}=1,z_{0}=0

\displaystyle y_{t+1}=x_{t}-bx_{t},

(67a)

\displaystyle z_{t+1}=\bm{z}_{t}-a(t+1)x_{t},

(67b)

\displaystyle(t+2)x_{t+1}=(t+1)y_{t+1}+z_{t+1}

(67c)

We can easily check that, for
t\geq 0
,

\displaystyle\begin{bmatrix}(t+1)y_{t+1}\\
z_{t+1}\end{bmatrix}=\Gamma^{t+1}\begin{bmatrix}1\\
0\end{bmatrix}.

(68)

Using the eigen decomposition of
\Gamma=U\Lambda U^{-1}
we can check that

\displaystyle U^{-1}\begin{bmatrix}1\\
0\end{bmatrix}

\displaystyle=\begin{bmatrix}-a&(1-r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{-}})r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{+}}\\
(1-r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{-}})r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{+}}&-r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{+}}r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{-}}\end{bmatrix}\begin{bmatrix}1\\
0\end{bmatrix}=\begin{bmatrix}-a\\
(1-r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{-}})r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{+}}\end{bmatrix},

\displaystyle U\Lambda^{t+1}U^{-1}\begin{bmatrix}1\\
0\end{bmatrix}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{{\scriptstyle\bm{\Delta}}}\begin{bmatrix}\frac{r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{-}}}{(1-r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{-}})}&1\\
1&\frac{1-r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{+}}}{r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{+}}}\end{bmatrix}\begin{bmatrix}-ar_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{+}}^{t+1}\\
(1-r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{-}})r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{+}}r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{-}}^{t+1}\end{bmatrix}=\frac{1}{{\scriptstyle\bm{\Delta}}}\begin{bmatrix}-r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{+}}r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{-}}\left[(1-r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{+}})r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{+}}^{t}-(1-r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{-}})r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{-}}^{t}\right]\\
-a\left[r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{+}}^{t+1}-r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{-}}^{t+1}\right]\end{bmatrix},

\displaystyle\Gamma^{t+1}\begin{bmatrix}1\\
0\end{bmatrix}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{{\scriptstyle\bm{\Delta}}}\begin{bmatrix}-r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{+}}r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{-}}\left[(1-r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{+}})r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{+}}^{t}-(1-r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{-}})r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{-}}^{t}\right]\\
-a\left[r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{+}}^{t+1}-r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{-}}^{t+1}\right].\end{bmatrix}

Hence,

\displaystyle\begin{bmatrix}(t+1)y_{t+1}\\
z_{t+1}\end{bmatrix}=\frac{1}{{\scriptstyle\bm{\Delta}}}\begin{bmatrix}-r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{+}}r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{-}}\left[(1-r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{+}})r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{+}}^{t}-(1-r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{-}})r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{-}}^{t}\right]\\
-a\left[r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{+}}^{t+1}-r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{-}}^{t+1}\right].\end{bmatrix}

(69)

Now use the potential function defined by
V_{t}=t^{2}y_{t}^{2}+\frac{1}{a}z_{t}^{2}
, for
t\geq 1
. If
0<a\leq b<1
then for
t\geq 1
we can show that
V_{t+1}\leq V_{t}
. Hence, for any
t\geq 1
,
V_{t}\leq V_{1}
. Note that
V_{1}<V_{0}
does not hold due to different initialization. From this,

\displaystyle(t+1)^{2}y_{t+1}^{2}\leq(t+1)^{2}y_{t+1}^{2}+\frac{1}{a}z_{t+1}^{2}\leq V_{1},

(70)

\displaystyle V_{1}=(1-b)^{2}+\frac{1}{a}a^{2}=(1-b)^{2}+a.

(71)

Using the expression of
(t+1)^{2}y_{t+1}^{2}
, we get

\displaystyle\left[\frac{\left(1-r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{+}}\right)r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{+}}^{t}-\left(1-r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{-}}\right)r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{-}}^{t}}{{\scriptstyle\bm{\Delta}}}\right]^{2}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{(r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{+}}r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{-}})^{2}}\left[(1-b)^{2}+a\right],

\displaystyle=1+\frac{a}{\left(1-b\right)^{2}}.

This proves the lemma.

Lemma 36

For
0<a\leq b<1
, for
\Gamma,\aleph
in Lemma
37
,

\displaystyle\left\langle{\begin{bmatrix}0&0\\
0&\displaystyle\frac{1}{a^{2}}\end{bmatrix}},{\Gamma^{T}\aleph\left(\Gamma^{T}\right)^{\top}}\right\rangle\leq 1+\frac{a}{\left(1-b\right)^{2}}.

(72)

Proof

In the following Lemma
37
, we compute the closed form for
\Gamma^{T}\aleph\left(\Gamma^{T}\right)^{\top}
=
\begin{bmatrix}\nu_{11}(t)&\nu_{12}(t)\\
\nu_{21}(t)&\nu_{22}(t)\end{bmatrix}
. Using this

\displaystyle\left\langle{\begin{bmatrix}0&0\\
0&\displaystyle\frac{1}{a^{2}}\end{bmatrix}},{\Gamma^{T}\aleph\left(\Gamma^{T}\right)^{\top}}\right\rangle=\frac{\nu_{22}(t)}{a^{2}}.

From Eq.(
76
),

\displaystyle\left\langle{\begin{bmatrix}0&0\\
0&\displaystyle\frac{1}{a^{2}}\end{bmatrix}},{\Gamma^{T}\aleph\left(\Gamma^{T}\right)^{\top}}\right\rangle=\left[\frac{\left(1-r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{+}}\right)r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{+}}^{t}-\left(1-r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{-}}\right)r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{-}}^{t}}{{\scriptstyle\bm{\Delta}}}\right]^{2}.

(73)

From Lemma
35
, the lemma holds.

Lemma 37

For
0<a,b<1
, with
\Gamma
and
\aleph
of form

\displaystyle\Gamma=\begin{bmatrix}1-b&1-b\\
-a&1-a\end{bmatrix},\qquad\aleph=\begin{bmatrix}b^{2}&ba\\
ba&a^{2}\end{bmatrix}.

The series

\displaystyle\sum_{t=0}^{\infty}\Gamma^{t}\aleph\left(\Gamma^{t}\right)^{\top}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{b(4-(a+2b))}\begin{bmatrix}2a+b(2b-3a)&a(2b-a)\\
a(2b-a)&2a^{2}\end{bmatrix}.

(74)

Proof

To calculate the exponents of
\Gamma
we use the eigen decomposition in Property
2
,

\displaystyle\Gamma

\displaystyle=\mathbf{U}\bm{\Lambda}\mathbf{U}^{-1},

\displaystyle\Gamma^{t}

\displaystyle=\mathbf{U}\bm{\Lambda}^{t}\mathbf{U}^{-1},

\displaystyle\Gamma^{t}\cdot\aleph\cdot\left(\Gamma^{t}\right)^{\top}

\displaystyle=\mathbf{U}\bm{\Lambda}^{t}\mathbf{U}^{-1}\cdot\aleph\cdot\left(\mathbf{U}\bm{\Lambda}^{t}\mathbf{U}^{-1}\right)^{\top},

From Property
2
that
\mathbf{U},\mathbf{U}^{-1}
are symmetric.

\displaystyle\Gamma^{t}\cdot\aleph\cdot\left(\Gamma^{t}\right)^{\top}

\displaystyle=\mathbf{U}\bm{\Lambda}^{t}\left[\mathbf{U}^{-1}\aleph\mathbf{U}^{-1}\right]\bm{\Lambda}^{t}\mathbf{U}.

Computing
{U^{-1}\aleph\mathbf{U}^{-1}}
:

From Property
2
,

\displaystyle\mathbf{U}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{{\scriptstyle\bm{\Delta}}}\begin{bmatrix}\frac{r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{-}}}{(1-r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{-}})}&1\\
1&\frac{1-r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{+}}}{r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{+}}}\end{bmatrix},\qquad\mathbf{U}^{-1}=\begin{bmatrix}-a&(1-r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{-}})r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{+}}\\
(1-r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{-}})r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{+}}&-r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{+}}r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{-}}\end{bmatrix},

\displaystyle\mathbf{U}^{-1}\aleph\mathbf{U}^{-1}

\displaystyle=\mathbf{U}^{-1}\begin{bmatrix}b^{2}&ab\\
ab&a^{2}\end{bmatrix}\mathbf{U}^{-1}=\mathbf{U}^{-1}\left(\begin{bmatrix}b\\
a\end{bmatrix}\right)\otimes\left(\begin{bmatrix}b\\
a\end{bmatrix}\right)\mathbf{U}^{-1},

\displaystyle=\left(\mathbf{U}^{-1}\begin{bmatrix}b\\
a\end{bmatrix}\right)\otimes\left(\mathbf{U}^{-1}\begin{bmatrix}b\\
a\end{bmatrix}\right),

\displaystyle\mathbf{U}\bm{\Lambda}^{t}\left[\mathbf{U}^{-1}\aleph\mathbf{U}^{-1}\right]\bm{\Lambda}^{t}\mathbf{U}

\displaystyle=\left(\mathbf{U}\bm{\Lambda}^{t}\mathbf{U}^{-1}\begin{bmatrix}b\\
a\end{bmatrix}\right)\otimes\left(\mathbf{U}\bm{\Lambda}^{t}\mathbf{U}^{-1}\begin{bmatrix}b\\
a\end{bmatrix}\right),

\displaystyle\mathbf{U}^{-1}\begin{bmatrix}b\\
a\end{bmatrix}

\displaystyle=\begin{bmatrix}-a&(1-r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{-}})r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{+}}\\
(1-r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{-}})r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{+}}&-r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{+}}r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{-}}\end{bmatrix}\begin{bmatrix}b\\
a\end{bmatrix}=\begin{bmatrix}-ab+(1-r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{-}})r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{+}}a\\
(1-r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{-}})r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{+}}b-r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{+}}r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{-}}a\end{bmatrix},

using
a=\left(1-r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{+}}\right)\left(1-r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{-}}\right),b=\left(1-r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{+}}r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{-}}\right)
,

\displaystyle=\begin{bmatrix}-a(1-r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{+}}r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{-}})+(1-r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{-}})r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{+}}a\\
(1-r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{-}})r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{+}}(1-r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{+}}r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{-}})-r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{+}}r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{-}}\left(1-r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{+}}\right)\left(1-r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{-}}\right)\end{bmatrix},

\displaystyle=\begin{bmatrix}a\left(-\left(1-r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{+}}r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{-}}\right)+\left(1-r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{-}}\right)r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{+}}\right)\\
r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{+}}(1-r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{-}})\left(1-r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{-}}r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{+}}-r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{-}}\left(1-r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{+}}\right)\right)\end{bmatrix}=\begin{bmatrix}a\left(r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{+}}-1\right)\\
r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{+}}(1-r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{-}})^{2}\end{bmatrix}.

\displaystyle\bm{\Lambda}^{t}\mathbf{U}^{-1}\begin{bmatrix}b\\
a\end{bmatrix}

\displaystyle=\begin{bmatrix}r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{+}}^{t}&0\\
0&r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{-}}^{t}\end{bmatrix}\begin{bmatrix}a\left(r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{+}}-1\right)\\
r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{+}}(1-r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{-}})^{2}\end{bmatrix}=\begin{bmatrix}ar_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{+}}^{t}\left(r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{+}}-1\right)\\
r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{+}}r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{-}}^{t}(1-r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{-}})^{2}\end{bmatrix}.

\displaystyle U\bm{\Lambda}^{t}\mathbf{U}^{-1}\begin{bmatrix}b\\
a\end{bmatrix}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{{\scriptstyle\bm{\Delta}}}\begin{bmatrix}\frac{r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{-}}}{(1-r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{-}})}&1\\
1&\frac{1-r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{+}}}{r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{+}}}\end{bmatrix}\begin{bmatrix}ar_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{+}}^{t}\left(r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{+}}-1\right)\\
r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{+}}r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{-}}^{t}(1-r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{-}})^{2}\end{bmatrix},

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{{\scriptstyle\bm{\Delta}}}\begin{bmatrix}\frac{r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{-}}}{(1-r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{-}})}ar_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{+}}^{t}\left(r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{+}}-1\right)+r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{+}}r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{-}}^{t}(1-r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{-}})^{2}\\
ar_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{+}}^{t}\left(r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{+}}-1\right)+\left(1-r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{+}}\right)r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{-}}^{t}(1-r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{-}})^{2}\end{bmatrix}.

Using
a=\left(1-r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{+}}\right)\left(1-r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{-}}\right),b=\left(1-r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{+}}r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{-}}\right),

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{{\scriptstyle\bm{\Delta}}}\begin{bmatrix}-r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{-}}r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{+}}^{t}\left(r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{+}}-1\right)^{2}+r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{+}}r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{-}}^{t}(1-r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{-}})^{2}\\
ar_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{+}}^{t}\left(r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{+}}-1\right)+ar_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{-}}^{t}(1-r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{-}})\end{bmatrix},

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{{\scriptstyle\bm{\Delta}}}\begin{bmatrix}-\left[r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{-}}\left(r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{+}}-1\right)^{2}r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{+}}^{t}-r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{+}}\left(1-r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{-}}\right)^{2}r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{-}}^{t}\right]\\
-a\left[\left(1-r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{+}}\right)r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{+}}^{t}-\left(1-r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{-}}\right)r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{-}}^{t}\right]\end{bmatrix}.

\displaystyle\Gamma^{t}\cdot\aleph\cdot\left(\Gamma^{t}\right)^{\top}

\displaystyle=\left(U\bm{\Lambda}^{t}\mathbf{U}^{-1}\begin{bmatrix}b\\
a\end{bmatrix}\right)\otimes\left(U\bm{\Lambda}^{t}\mathbf{U}^{-1}\begin{bmatrix}b\\
a\end{bmatrix}\right),

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{{\scriptstyle\bm{\Delta}}}\begin{bmatrix}-\left[r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{-}}\left(1-r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{+}}\right)^{2}r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{+}}^{t}-r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{+}}\left(1-r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{-}}\right)^{2}r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{-}}^{t}\right]\\
-a\left[\left(1-r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{+}}\right)r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{+}}^{t}-\left(1-r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{-}}\right)r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{-}}^{t}\right]\end{bmatrix}\otimes\frac{1}{{\scriptstyle\bm{\Delta}}}\begin{bmatrix}-\left[r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{-}}\left(r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{+}}-1\right)^{2}r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{+}}^{t}-r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{+}}\left(1-r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{-}}\right)^{2}r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{-}}^{t}\right]\\
-a\left[\left(1-r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{+}}\right)r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{+}}^{t}-\left(1-r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{-}}\right)r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{-}}^{t}\right]\end{bmatrix},

\displaystyle=\begin{bmatrix}\nu_{11}(t)&\nu_{12}(t)\\
\nu_{12}(t)&\nu_{22}(t)\end{bmatrix}.

where

\displaystyle{\scriptstyle\bm{\Delta}}^{2}~\nu_{11}(t)

\displaystyle\overset{\scriptstyle\mathrm{def}}{=}\left[r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{-}}\left(1-r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{+}}\right)^{2}r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{+}}^{t}-r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{+}}\left(1-r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{-}}\right)^{2}r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{-}}^{t}\right]^{2},

(75)

\displaystyle{\scriptstyle\bm{\Delta}}^{2}~\nu_{22}(t)

\displaystyle\overset{\scriptstyle\mathrm{def}}{=}a^{2}\left[\left(1-r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{+}}\right)r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{+}}^{t}-\left(1-r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{-}}\right)r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{-}}^{t}\right]^{2},

(76)

\displaystyle{\scriptstyle\bm{\Delta}}^{2}~\nu_{12}(t)

\displaystyle\overset{\scriptstyle\mathrm{def}}{=}a\left(r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{-}}\left(1-r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{+}}\right)^{2}r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{+}}^{t}-r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{+}}\left(1-r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{-}}\right)^{2}r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{-}}^{t}\right)\left(\left(1-r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{+}}\right)r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{+}}^{t}-\left(1-r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{-}}\right)r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{-}}^{t}\right).

(77)

Using these,

\displaystyle\sum\limits_{t=0}^{\infty}\Gamma^{t}\cdot\aleph\cdot\left(\Gamma^{t}\right)^{\top}=\begin{bmatrix}\sum\limits_{t=0}^{\infty}\nu_{11}(t)&\sum\limits_{t=0}^{\infty}\nu_{12}(t)\\
\sum\limits_{t=0}^{\infty}\nu_{12}(t)&\sum\limits_{t=0}^{\infty}\nu_{22}(t)\end{bmatrix}.

Evaluating
\sum\limits_{t=0}^{\infty}\nu_{11}(t)
:

\displaystyle{\scriptstyle\bm{\Delta}}^{2}~\sum_{t=0}^{\infty}~\nu_{11}(t)

\displaystyle=\sum_{t=0}^{\infty}\left[r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{-}}\left(1-r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{+}}\right)^{2}r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{+}}^{t}-r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{+}}\left(1-r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{-}}\right)^{2}r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{-}}^{t}\right]^{2},

\displaystyle=\left[r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{-}}\left(1-r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{+}}\right)^{2}-r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{+}}\left(1-r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{-}}\right)^{2}\right]^{2}

\displaystyle\hskip 28.45274pt+\sum_{t=1}^{\infty}\left[r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{-}}^{2}\left(1-r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{+}}\right)^{4}r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{+}}^{2t}+r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{+}}^{2}\left(1-r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{-}}\right)^{4}r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{-}}^{2t}-2r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{-}}\left(1-r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{+}}\right)^{2}r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{+}}\left(1-r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{-}}\right)^{2}r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{+}}^{t}r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{-}}^{t}\right].

From Property
2
, when
0<a,b<1
then
|r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{+}}|,|r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{-}}|<1
. Hence, the following holds,

\displaystyle\sum_{t=1}^{\infty}r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{+}}^{2t}

\displaystyle=\frac{r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{+}}^{2}}{1-r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{+}}^{2}},\quad\sum_{t=1}^{\infty}r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{-}}^{2t}=\frac{r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{-}}^{2}}{1-r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{-}}^{2}},\quad\sum_{t=1}^{\infty}r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{+}}^{t}r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{-}}^{t}=\frac{r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{+}}r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{-}}}{1-r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{+}}r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{-}}},

\displaystyle r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{-}}\left(1-r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{+}}\right)^{2}-r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{+}}\left(1-r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{-}}\right)^{2}

\displaystyle=\left(1-(1-r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{-}})\right)\left(1-r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{+}}\right)^{2}-\left(1-(1-r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{+}})\right)\left(1-r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{-}}\right)^{2}

\displaystyle=\left(1-r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{+}}\right)^{2}-\left(1-r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{-}}\right)^{2}-(1-r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{-}})\left(1-r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{+}}\right)^{2}+(1-r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{+}})\left(1-r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{-}}\right)^{2},

\displaystyle=\left(r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{-}}-r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{+}}\right)\left(2-r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{-}}-r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{+}}\right)-(1-r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{-}})\left(1-r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{+}}\right)\left(r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{-}}-r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{+}}\right),

\displaystyle=\left(r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{-}}-r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{+}}\right)\left[\left(2-r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{-}}-r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{+}}\right)-(1-r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{-}})\left(1-r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{+}}\right)\right],

\displaystyle=\left(r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{-}}-r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{+}}\right)\left[1-r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{+}}r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{-}}\right]=-{\scriptstyle\bm{\Delta}}b.

From here, the first term of the sumation is as follows,

\displaystyle r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{-}}\left(1-r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{+}}\right)^{2}-r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{+}}\left(1-r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{-}}\right)^{2}

\displaystyle=-{\scriptstyle\bm{\Delta}}b.

(78)

To calculate the sum of the remaining terms,

\displaystyle{\scriptstyle\bm{\Delta}}^{2}~\left(\sum_{t=0}^{\infty}\nu_{11}(t)-b^{2}\right)

\displaystyle=\sum_{t=1}^{\infty}\left[r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{-}}^{2}\left(1-r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{+}}\right)^{4}r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{+}}^{2t}+r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{+}}^{2}\left(1-r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{-}}\right)^{4}r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{-}}^{2t}-2r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{-}}\left(1-r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{+}}\right)^{2}r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{+}}\left(1-r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{-}}\right)^{2}r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{+}}^{t}r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{-}}^{t}\right],

\displaystyle=r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{+}}^{2}r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{-}}^{2}\sum_{t=0}^{\infty}\left[\left(1-r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{+}}\right)^{4}r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{+}}^{2t}+\left(1-r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{-}}\right)^{4}r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{-}}^{2t}-2r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{-}}\left(1-r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{+}}\right)^{2}r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{+}}\left(1-r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{-}}\right)^{2}r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{+}}^{t}r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{-}}^{t}\right],

\displaystyle=r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{+}}^{2}r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{-}}^{2}\sum_{t=0}^{\infty}\left[r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{-}}^{2}\left(1-r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{+}}\right)^{2}r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{+}}^{t}-\left(1-r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{-}}\right)^{2}r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{-}}^{t}\right]^{2}.

Invoking Lemma
39
,

\displaystyle{\scriptstyle\bm{\Delta}}^{2}~\left(\sum_{t=0}^{\infty}\nu_{11}(t)-b^{2}\right)

\displaystyle={\scriptstyle\bm{\Delta}}^{2}r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{+}}^{2}r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{-}}^{2}\left[\frac{a(4-(a+2b))+(a+2b)^{2}}{2b(4-(a+2b))}\right],

\displaystyle\left(\sum_{t=0}^{\infty}\nu_{11}(t)-b^{2}\right)

\displaystyle=(1-b)^{2}\left[\frac{a(4-(a+2b))+(a+2b)^{2}}{2b(4-(a+2b))}\right].

Using simple algebraic manipulations summation of
\nu_{11}(t)
’s can be compactly written as follows,

\displaystyle\sum_{t=0}^{\infty}\nu_{11}(t)

\displaystyle=b^{2}+(1-b)^{2}\left[\frac{a(4-(a+2b))+(a+2b)^{2}}{2b(4-(a+2b))}\right],

\displaystyle=b^{2}+(1-b)^{2}\left[\frac{4a+(a+2b)\left((a+2b)-a\right)}{2b(4-(a+2b))}\right],

\displaystyle=b^{2}+(1-b)^{2}\left[\frac{4a+2b(a+2b)}{2b(4-(a+2b))}\right],

\displaystyle=b^{2}+\frac{(a+2b)(1-b)^{2}}{(4-(a+2b))}+\left[\frac{4a(1-b)^{2}}{2b(4-(a+2b))}\right],

\displaystyle=\frac{b^{2}(4-(a+2b))+(a+2b)(1-b)^{2}}{(4-(a+2b))}+\left[\frac{4a(1-b)^{2}}{2b(4-(a+2b))}\right].

\displaystyle b^{2}(4-(a+2b))+(a+2b)(1-b)^{2}

\displaystyle=b^{2}(4-(a+2b))+(a+2b)\left(1-2b+b^{2}\right)

\displaystyle=4b^{2}+(a+2b)\left(1-2b\right)=a+2b-2ab,

\displaystyle\sum_{t=0}^{\infty}\nu_{11}(t)

\displaystyle=\frac{a+2b-2ab}{(4-(a+2b))}+\left[\frac{2a(1-b)^{2}}{b(4-(a+2b))}\right],

\displaystyle=\frac{b(a+2b-2ab)+2a(1-b)^{2}}{b(4-(a+2b))}

\displaystyle=\frac{ab+2b^{2}-2ab^{2}+2a-4ab+2ab^{2}}{b(4-(a+2b))}=\frac{2a+b(2b-3a)}{b(4-(a+2b))}.

Hence,

\displaystyle\sum_{t=0}^{\infty}\nu_{11}(t)

\displaystyle=\frac{2a}{b(4-(a+2b))}+\frac{2b-3a}{4-(a+2b)}.

(79)

Evaluating
\sum\limits_{t=0}^{\infty}\nu_{22}(t)
:

From Eq.(
77
),

\displaystyle{\scriptstyle\bm{\Delta}}^{2}~\nu_{22}(t)

\displaystyle=a^{2}\left[\left(1-r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{+}}\right)r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{+}}^{t}-\left(1-r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{-}}\right)r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{-}}^{t}\right]^{2},

\displaystyle{\scriptstyle\bm{\Delta}}^{2}~\sum_{t=0}^{\infty}\ \nu_{22}(t)

\displaystyle=a^{2}\sum_{t=0}^{\infty}\left(1-r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{+}}\right)^{2}r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{+}}^{2t}+\left(1-r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{-}}\right)^{2}r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{-}}^{2t}-2\left(1-r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{+}}\right)\left(1-r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{-}}\right)r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{+}}^{t}r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{-}}^{t}.

From Property
2
, when
0<a,b<1
then
|r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{+}}|,|r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{-}}|<1
. Hence, the following holds,

\displaystyle\sum_{t=0}^{\infty}r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{+}}^{2t}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{1-r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{+}}^{2}},\quad\sum_{t=0}^{\infty}r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{-}}^{2t}=\frac{1}{1-r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{-}}^{2}},\quad\sum_{t=0}^{\infty}r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{+}}^{t}r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{-}}^{t}=\frac{1}{1-r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{+}}r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{-}}},

\displaystyle{\scriptstyle\bm{\Delta}}^{2}~\sum_{t=0}^{\infty}\ \nu_{22}(t)

\displaystyle=a^{2}\left[\left(1-r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{+}}\right)^{2}\frac{1}{1-r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{+}}^{2}}+\left(1-r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{-}}\right)^{2}\frac{1}{1-r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{-}}^{2}}-2\frac{\left(1-r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{+}}\right)\left(1-r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{-}}\right)}{1-r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{+}}r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{-}}}\right].

\displaystyle=a^{2}\left[\frac{1-r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{+}}}{1+r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{+}}}+\frac{1-r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{-}}}{1+r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{-}}}-2\frac{\left(1-r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{+}}\right)\left(1-r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{-}}\right)}{1-r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{+}}r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{-}}}\right].

\displaystyle{\scriptstyle\bm{\Delta}}^{2}~\sum_{t=0}^{\infty}\nu_{22}(t)

\displaystyle=a^{2}\left[\frac{1-r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{+}}}{1+r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{+}}}+\frac{1-r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{-}}}{1+r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{-}}}-2\frac{\left(1-r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{+}}\right)\left(1-r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{-}}\right)}{1-r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{+}}r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{-}}}\right].

(80)

Considering the computation in the right part,

\displaystyle\frac{1-r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{+}}}{1+r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{+}}}+\frac{1-r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{-}}}{1+r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{-}}}

\displaystyle=\frac{\left(1-r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{+}}\right)\left(1+r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{-}}\right)+\left(1-r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{-}}\right)\left(1+r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{+}}\right)}{\left(1+r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{+}}\right)\left(1+r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{-}}\right)}=\frac{2\left(1-r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{+}}r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{-}}\right)}{\left(1+r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{+}}\right)\left(1+r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{-}}\right)},

\displaystyle\frac{1-r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{+}}}{1+r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{+}}}+\frac{1-r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{-}}}{1+r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{-}}}-2\frac{\left(1-r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{+}}\right)\left(1-r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{-}}\right)}{1-r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{+}}r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{-}}}

\displaystyle=\frac{2\left(1-r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{+}}r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{-}}\right)}{\left(1+r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{+}}\right)\left(1+r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{-}}\right)}-2\frac{\left(1-r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{+}}\right)\left(1-r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{-}}\right)}{1-r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{+}}r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{-}}}

\displaystyle=2\frac{\left(1-r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{+}}r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{-}}\right)^{2}-\left(1-r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{+}}^{2}\right)\left(1-r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{-}}^{2}\right)}{\left(1+r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{+}}\right)\left(1+r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{-}}\right)\left(1-r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{+}}r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{-}}\right)}.

Computing the numerator, we get the following,

\displaystyle\left(1-r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{+}}r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{-}}\right)^{2}-\left(1-r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{+}}^{2}\right)\left(1-r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{-}}^{2}\right)

\displaystyle=1-2r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{+}}r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{-}}+r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{+}}^{2}r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{-}}^{2}-\left(1-r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{+}}^{2}-r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{-}}^{2}+r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{+}}^{2}r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{-}}^{2}\right),

\displaystyle=r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{+}}^{2}+r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{-}}^{2}-2r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{+}}r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{-}}={\scriptstyle\bm{\Delta}}^{2}.

The denominator from Eq.(
94
),

\displaystyle\left(1+r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{-}}\right)\left(1+r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{+}}\right)\left(1-r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{+}}r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{-}}\right)

\displaystyle=b(4-(a+2b)).

Substituting these back in Eq.(
80
), we get

\displaystyle{\scriptstyle\bm{\Delta}}^{2}~\sum_{t=0}^{\infty}\nu_{22}(t)

\displaystyle=\frac{2a^{2}{\scriptstyle\bm{\Delta}}^{2}}{b(4-(a+2b))}.

Hence,

\displaystyle\sum_{t=0}^{\infty}\nu_{22}(t)

\displaystyle=\frac{2a^{2}}{b(4-(a+2b))}.

(81)

Evaluating
\sum\limits_{t=0}^{\infty}\nu_{12}(t)
:

From Eq.(
77
),

\displaystyle{\scriptstyle\bm{\Delta}}^{2}~\nu_{12}(t)

\displaystyle=a\left(r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{-}}\left(1-r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{+}}\right)^{2}r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{+}}^{t}-r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{+}}\left(1-r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{-}}\right)^{2}r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{-}}^{t}\right)\left(\left(1-r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{+}}\right)r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{+}}^{t}-\left(1-r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{-}}\right)r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{-}}^{t}\right),

\displaystyle=a\left[r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{-}}\left(1-r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{+}}\right)^{3}r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{+}}^{2t}+r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{+}}\left(1-r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{-}}\right)^{3}r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{-}}^{2t}-\left\{r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{-}}\left(1-r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{+}}\right)+r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{+}}\left(1-r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{-}}\right)\right\}\left(1-r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{+}}\right)\left(1-r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{-}}\right)r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{+}}^{t}r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{-}}^{t}\right],

\displaystyle{\scriptstyle\bm{\Delta}}^{2}~\sum_{t=0}^{\infty}\nu_{12}(t)

\displaystyle=a\sum_{t=0}^{\infty}\left[r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{-}}\left(1-r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{+}}\right)^{3}r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{+}}^{2t}+r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{+}}\left(1-r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{-}}\right)^{3}r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{-}}^{2t}-\left\{r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{-}}\left(1-r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{+}}\right)+r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{+}}\left(1-r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{-}}\right)\right\}\left(1-r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{+}}\right)\left(1-r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{-}}\right)r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{+}}^{t}r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{-}}^{t}\right].

For
0<a,b<1
, we have
|r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{+}}|,|r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{-}}|<1
from Property
2
. Hence, the following holds,

\displaystyle\sum_{t=0}^{\infty}r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{+}}^{2t}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{1-r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{+}}^{2}},\quad\sum_{t=0}^{\infty}r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{-}}^{2t}=\frac{1}{1-r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{-}}^{2}},\quad\sum_{t=0}^{\infty}r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{+}}^{t}r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{-}}^{t}=\frac{1}{1-r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{+}}r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{-}}}.

\displaystyle=a\left[\frac{r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{-}}\left(1-r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{+}}\right)^{3}}{1-r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{+}}^{2}}+\frac{r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{+}}\left(1-r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{-}}\right)^{3}}{1-r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{-}}^{2}}-\left\{r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{-}}\left(1-r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{+}}\right)+r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{+}}\left(1-r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{-}}\right)\right\}\frac{\left(1-r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{+}}\right)\left(1-r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{-}}\right)}{1-r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{+}}r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{-}}}\right].

\displaystyle=a\left[\frac{r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{-}}\left(1-r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{+}}\right)^{2}}{1+r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{+}}}+\frac{r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{+}}\left(1-r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{-}}\right)^{2}}{1+r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{-}}}-\left\{r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{-}}\left(1-r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{+}}\right)+r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{+}}\left(1-r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{-}}\right)\right\}\frac{\left(1-r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{+}}\right)\left(1-r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{-}}\right)}{1-r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{+}}r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{-}}}\right].

Using

\displaystyle 2\left(1-r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{+}}r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{-}}\right)

\displaystyle=\left(1-r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{-}}\right)\left(1+r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{+}}\right)+\left(1-r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{+}}\right)\left(1+r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{-}}\right),

\displaystyle 2r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{-}}\left(1+r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{-}}\right)\left(1-r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{+}}\right)^{2}\left(1-r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{+}}r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{-}}\right)

\displaystyle=r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{-}}\left(1-r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{+}}\right)\left(1-r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{-}}^{2}\right)\left(1-r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{+}}^{2}\right)+r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{-}}\left(1+r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{-}}\right)^{2}\left(1-r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{+}}\right)^{3}.

(82)

Similarly by symmetry

\displaystyle 2r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{+}}\left(1+r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{+}}\right)\left(1-r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{-}}\right)^{2}\left(1-r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{+}}r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{-}}\right)

\displaystyle=r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{+}}\left(1-r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{-}}\right)\left(1-r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{-}}^{2}\right)\left(1-r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{+}}^{2}\right)+r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{+}}\left(1+r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{+}}\right)^{2}\left(1-r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{-}}\right)^{3}.

(83)

\displaystyle\begin{aligned} \left[r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{-}}\left(1-r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{+}}\right)+r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{+}}\left(1-r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{-}}\right)\right]&\left(1-r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{+}}^{2}\right)\left(1-r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{-}}^{2}\right),\\
&\hskip-42.67912pt=r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{-}}\left(1-r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{+}}\right)\left(1-r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{+}}^{2}\right)\left(1-r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{-}}^{2}\right)+r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{+}}\left(1-r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{-}}\right)\left(1-r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{+}}^{2}\right)\left(1-r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{-}}^{2}\right).\end{aligned}

(84)

Combining them,

\displaystyle\textrm{Eq}.(\ref{lem:inv:12-p1})+\textrm{Eq}.(\ref{lem:inv:12-p2})-2*\textrm{Eq}.(\ref{lem:inv:12-p3})

\displaystyle=r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{-}}\left(1+r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{-}}\right)^{2}\left(1-r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{+}}\right)^{3}+r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{+}}\left(1+r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{+}}\right)^{2}\left(1-r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{-}}\right)^{3}

\displaystyle\hskip 28.45274pt-r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{-}}\left(1-r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{+}}\right)\left(1-r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{+}}^{2}\right)\left(1-r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{-}}^{2}\right)-r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{+}}\left(1-r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{-}}\right)\left(1-r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{+}}^{2}\right)\left(1-r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{-}}^{2}\right),

\displaystyle=r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{-}}\left(1+r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{-}}\right)^{2}\left(1-r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{+}}\right)^{3}-r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{-}}\left(1-r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{+}}\right)\left(1-r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{+}}^{2}\right)\left(1-r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{-}}^{2}\right),

\displaystyle\hskip 28.45274pt+r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{+}}\left(1+r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{+}}\right)^{2}\left(1-r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{-}}\right)^{3}-r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{+}}\left(1-r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{-}}\right)\left(1-r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{+}}^{2}\right)\left(1-r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{-}}^{2}\right),

\displaystyle=r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{-}}\left(1+r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{-}}\right)\left(1-r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{+}}\right)^{2}\left[\left(1+r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{-}}\right)\left(1-r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{+}}\right)-\left(1+r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{+}}\right)\left(1-r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{-}}\right)\right],

\displaystyle\hskip 28.45274pt+r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{+}}\left(1+r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{+}}\right)\left(1-r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{-}}\right)^{2}\left[\left(1+r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{+}}\right)\left(1-r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{-}}\right)-\left(1-r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{+}}\right)\left(1+r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{-}}\right)\right],

\displaystyle=2r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{-}}\left(1+r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{-}}\right)\left(1-r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{+}}\right)^{2}\left[r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{-}}-r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{+}}\right]+2r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{+}}\left(1+r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{+}}\right)\left(1-r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{-}}\right)^{2}\left[r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{+}}-r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{-}}\right],

\displaystyle=-2{\scriptstyle\bm{\Delta}}\left[r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{-}}\left(1+r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{-}}\right)\left(1-r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{+}}\right)^{2}-r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{+}}\left(1+r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{+}}\right)\left(1-r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{-}}\right)^{2}\right].

Evaluating

\displaystyle r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{-}}\left(1+r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{-}}\right)\left(1-r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{+}}\right)^{2}-r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{+}}\left(1+r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{+}}\right)\left(1-r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{-}}\right)^{2}

\displaystyle=r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{-}}\left(1-r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{+}}\right)^{2}-r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{+}}\left(1-r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{-}}\right)^{2}+r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{-}}^{2}\left(1-r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{+}}\right)^{2}-r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{+}}^{2}\left(1-r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{-}}\right)^{2}.

From Eq.(
78
), we have the following,

\displaystyle r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{-}}\left(1-r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{+}}\right)^{2}-r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{+}}\left(1-r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{-}}\right)^{2}

\displaystyle=-{\scriptstyle\bm{\Delta}}b,

\displaystyle r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{-}}^{2}\left(1-r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{+}}\right)^{2}-r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{+}}^{2}\left(1-r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{-}}\right)^{2}

\displaystyle=\left[r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{-}}\left(1-r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{+}}\right)-r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{+}}\left(1-r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{-}}\right)\right]\left[r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{-}}\left(1-r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{+}}\right)+r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{+}}\left(1-r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{-}}\right)\right],

\displaystyle=-{\scriptstyle\bm{\Delta}}\left[r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{+}}+r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{-}}-2r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{+}}r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{-}}\right]=-{\scriptstyle\bm{\Delta}}\left[2-(a+b)-2(1-b)\right],

\displaystyle=-{\scriptstyle\bm{\Delta}}\left[b-a\right].

\displaystyle r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{-}}\left(1+r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{-}}\right)\left(1-r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{+}}\right)^{2}-r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{+}}\left(1+r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{+}}\right)\left(1-r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{-}}\right)^{2}

\displaystyle=-{\scriptstyle\bm{\Delta}}b-{\scriptstyle\bm{\Delta}}\left[b-a\right]=-{\scriptstyle\bm{\Delta}}\left(2b-a\right).

So the numerator of
{\scriptstyle\bm{\Delta}}^{2}\sum\limits_{t=0}^{\infty}\nu_{12}(t)

\displaystyle\frac{a}{2}\left(\textrm{Eq}.(\ref{lem:inv:12-p1})+\textrm{Eq}.(\ref{lem:inv:12-p2})-2*\textrm{Eq}.(\ref{lem:inv:12-p3})\right)

\displaystyle=\frac{a}{2}\left(-2{\scriptstyle\bm{\Delta}}(-{\scriptstyle\bm{\Delta}}\left(2b-a\right))\right),

\displaystyle={\scriptstyle\bm{\Delta}}^{2}a\left(2b-a\right).

the denominator is

\displaystyle\left(1+r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{-}}\right)\left(1+r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{+}}\right)\left(1-r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{+}}r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{-}}\right)

\displaystyle=b(4-(a+2b)).

Finally, we have

\displaystyle\sum\limits_{t=0}^{\infty}\nu_{12}(t)=\frac{a(2b-a)}{b(4-(a+2b))}.

(85)

From Eq.(
85
), Eq.(
81
), Eq.(
79
)

\displaystyle\sum_{t=0}^{\infty}\Gamma^{t}\aleph\left(\Gamma^{t}\right)^{\top}

\displaystyle=\begin{bmatrix}\displaystyle\frac{2a}{b(4-(a+2b))}+\frac{2b-3a}{4-(a+2b)}&\displaystyle\frac{a(2b-a)}{b(4-(a+2b))}\\
\displaystyle\frac{a(2b-a)}{b(4-(a+2b))}&\displaystyle\frac{2a^{2}}{b(4-(a+2b))}\end{bmatrix}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{b(4-(a+2b))}\begin{bmatrix}2a+b(2b-3a)&a(2b-a)\\
a(2b-a)&2a^{2}\end{bmatrix}.

This proves the lemma.

Lemma 38

For
0<a,b<1
, with
\Gamma
and
\aleph
of form

\displaystyle\Gamma=\begin{bmatrix}1-b&1-b\\
-a&1-a\end{bmatrix},\qquad\aleph=\begin{bmatrix}1&1\\
1&1\end{bmatrix}.

The series

\displaystyle\begin{bmatrix}b&a\\
b&a\end{bmatrix}\sum_{t=0}^{\infty}\left(\Gamma^{t}\right)^{\top}\aleph\Gamma^{t}\begin{bmatrix}b&b\\
a&a\end{bmatrix}

\displaystyle=\left(\frac{2a}{b(4-(a+2b))}+\frac{a+2b}{(4-(a+2b))}\right)\begin{bmatrix}1&1\\
1&1\end{bmatrix}.

(86)

Proof

To calculate the exponents of
\Gamma
we use the eigendecomposition from Property
2
,

\displaystyle\Gamma

\displaystyle=\mathbf{U}\bm{\Lambda}\mathbf{U}^{-1},

\displaystyle\Gamma^{t}

\displaystyle=\mathbf{U}\bm{\Lambda}^{t}\mathbf{U}^{-1},

\displaystyle\left(\Gamma^{t}\right)^{\top}\cdot\aleph\cdot\Gamma^{t}

\displaystyle=\mathbf{U}^{-1}\bm{\Lambda}^{t}\mathbf{U}\cdot\aleph\cdot\left(\mathbf{U}^{-1}\bm{\Lambda}^{t}\mathbf{U}\right)^{\top}.

Using the fact in Property
2
, that
\mathbf{U},\mathbf{U}^{-1}
are symmetric.

\displaystyle\begin{bmatrix}b&a\\
b&a\end{bmatrix}\left(\Gamma^{t}\right)^{\top}\cdot\aleph\cdot\Gamma^{t}\begin{bmatrix}b&a\\
b&a\end{bmatrix}^{\top}

\displaystyle=\begin{bmatrix}b&a\\
b&a\end{bmatrix}\mathbf{U}^{-1}\bm{\Lambda}^{t}\left[\mathbf{U}\aleph\mathbf{U}\right]\bm{\Lambda}^{t}\mathbf{U}^{-1}\begin{bmatrix}b&a\\
b&a\end{bmatrix}^{\top}.

\displaystyle\mathbf{U}\aleph\mathbf{U}

\displaystyle=\mathbf{U}\begin{bmatrix}1&1\\
1&1\end{bmatrix}\mathbf{U}=\mathbf{U}\left(\begin{bmatrix}1\\
1\end{bmatrix}\right)\otimes\left(\begin{bmatrix}1\\
1\end{bmatrix}\right)\mathbf{U},

\displaystyle=\left(\mathbf{U}\begin{bmatrix}1\\
1\end{bmatrix}\right)\otimes\left(\mathbf{U}\begin{bmatrix}1\\
1\end{bmatrix}\right),

\displaystyle\mathbf{U}^{-1}\bm{\Lambda}^{t}\left[\mathbf{U}\aleph\mathbf{U}\right]\bm{\Lambda}^{t}\mathbf{U}^{-1}

\displaystyle=\left(\mathbf{U}^{-1}\bm{\Lambda}^{t}\mathbf{U}\begin{bmatrix}1\\
1\end{bmatrix}\right)\otimes\left(\mathbf{U}^{-1}\bm{\Lambda}^{t}\mathbf{U}\begin{bmatrix}1\\
1\end{bmatrix}\right),

\displaystyle\begin{bmatrix}b&a\\
b&a\end{bmatrix}\left(\Gamma^{t}\right)^{\top}\cdot\aleph\cdot\Gamma^{t}\begin{bmatrix}b&a\\
b&a\end{bmatrix}^{\top}

\displaystyle=\left(\begin{bmatrix}b&a\\
b&a\end{bmatrix}\mathbf{U}^{-1}\bm{\Lambda}^{t}\mathbf{U}\begin{bmatrix}1\\
1\end{bmatrix}\right)\otimes\left(\begin{bmatrix}b&a\\
b&a\end{bmatrix}\mathbf{U}^{-1}\bm{\Lambda}^{t}\mathbf{U}\begin{bmatrix}1\\
1\end{bmatrix}\right).

From eigendecomposition given in Property
2
,

\displaystyle{\scriptstyle\bm{\Delta}}\mathbf{U}

\displaystyle=\begin{bmatrix}\frac{r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{-}}}{(1-r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{-}})}&1\\
1&\frac{1-r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{+}}}{r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{+}}}\end{bmatrix},

\displaystyle{\scriptstyle\bm{\Delta}}\mathbf{U}\begin{bmatrix}1\\
1\end{bmatrix}

\displaystyle=\begin{bmatrix}\frac{r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{-}}}{(1-r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{-}})}&1\\
1&\frac{1-r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{+}}}{r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{+}}}\end{bmatrix}\begin{bmatrix}b\\
a\end{bmatrix}=\begin{bmatrix}\displaystyle\frac{1}{(1-r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{-}})}\\
\displaystyle\frac{1}{r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{+}}}\end{bmatrix},

\displaystyle{\scriptstyle\bm{\Delta}}\bm{\Lambda}^{t}\mathbf{U}\begin{bmatrix}1\\
1\end{bmatrix}

\displaystyle=\begin{bmatrix}r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{+}}^{t}&0\\
0&r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{-}}^{t}\end{bmatrix}\begin{bmatrix}\displaystyle\frac{1}{(1-r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{-}})}\\
\displaystyle\frac{1}{r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{+}}}\end{bmatrix}=\begin{bmatrix}\displaystyle\frac{r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{+}}^{t}}{(1-r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{-}})}\\
\displaystyle\frac{r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{-}}^{t}}{r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{+}}}\end{bmatrix}.

Again from Property
2
using
\mathbf{U}^{-1}

\displaystyle\mathbf{U}^{-1}

\displaystyle=\begin{bmatrix}-a&(1-r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{-}})r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{+}}\\
(1-r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{-}})r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{+}}&-r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{+}}r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{-}}\end{bmatrix},

\displaystyle{\scriptstyle\bm{\Delta}}\mathbf{U}^{-1}\bm{\Lambda}^{t}\mathbf{U}\begin{bmatrix}1\\
1\end{bmatrix}

\displaystyle=\begin{bmatrix}-a&(1-r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{-}})r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{+}}\\
(1-r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{-}})r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{+}}&-r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{+}}r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{-}}\end{bmatrix}\begin{bmatrix}\frac{r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{+}}^{t}}{(1-r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{-}})}\\
\frac{r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{-}}^{t}}{r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{+}}}\end{bmatrix},

\displaystyle=\begin{bmatrix}\frac{-ar_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{+}}^{t}}{(1-r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{-}})}+(1-r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{-}})r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{-}}^{t}\\
r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{+}}^{t+1}-r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{-}}^{t+1}\end{bmatrix}.

Using
a=\left(1-r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{+}}\right)\left(1-r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{-}}\right)
,

\displaystyle{\scriptstyle\bm{\Delta}}\mathbf{U}^{-1}\bm{\Lambda}^{t}\mathbf{U}\begin{bmatrix}1\\
1\end{bmatrix}

\displaystyle=\begin{bmatrix}-(1-r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{+}})r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{+}}^{t}+(1-r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{-}})r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{-}}^{t}\\
r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{+}}^{t+1}-r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{-}}^{t+1}\end{bmatrix}=\begin{bmatrix}-\left[(1-r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{+}})r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{+}}^{t}-(1-r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{-}})r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{-}}^{t}\right]\\
r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{+}}^{t+1}-r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{-}}^{t+1}\end{bmatrix}.

\displaystyle{\scriptstyle\bm{\Delta}}\begin{bmatrix}b&a\\
b&a\end{bmatrix}\mathbf{U}^{-1}\bm{\Lambda}^{t}\mathbf{U}\begin{bmatrix}1\\
1\end{bmatrix}

\displaystyle=\begin{bmatrix}b&a\\
b&a\end{bmatrix}\begin{bmatrix}-\left[(1-r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{+}})r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{+}}^{t}-(1-r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{-}})r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{-}}^{t}\right]\\
r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{+}}^{t+1}-r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{-}}^{t+1}\end{bmatrix},

\displaystyle=\begin{bmatrix}-b\left((1-r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{+}})r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{+}}^{t}-(1-r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{-}})r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{-}}^{t}\right)+a\left(r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{+}}^{t+1}-r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{-}}^{t+1}\right)\\
-b\left((1-r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{+}})r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{+}}^{t}-(1-r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{-}})r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{-}}^{t}\right)+a\left(r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{+}}^{t+1}-r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{-}}^{t+1}\right)\end{bmatrix},

\displaystyle=\begin{bmatrix}-\left[\left(b(1-r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{+}})-a(r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{+}})\right)r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{+}}^{t}-\left(b(1-r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{-}})-a(r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{-}})\right)r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{-}}^{t}\right]\\
-\left[\left(b(1-r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{+}})-a(r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{+}})\right)r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{+}}^{t}-\left(b(1-r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{-}})-a(r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{-}})\right)r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{-}}^{t}\right].\end{bmatrix}

Using
a=\left(1-r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{+}}\right)\left(1-r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{-}}\right),b=1-r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{-}}r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{+}}
,

\displaystyle b(1-r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{+}})-a(r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{+}})

\displaystyle=(1-r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{+}}r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{-}})(1-r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{+}})-\left(1-r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{+}}\right)\left(1-r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{-}}\right)r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{+}},

\displaystyle=\left(1-r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{+}}\right)\left((1-r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{+}}r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{-}})-r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{+}}\left(1-r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{-}}\right)\right),

\displaystyle=\left(1-r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{+}}\right)\left(1-r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{+}}r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{-}}-r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{+}}+r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{+}}r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{-}}\right)=\left(1-r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{+}}\right)^{2}.

By symmetry,

\displaystyle b(1-r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{-}})-a(r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{-}})

\displaystyle=\left(1-r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{-}}\right)^{2}.

Substituting this back we get

\displaystyle{\scriptstyle\bm{\Delta}}\begin{bmatrix}b&a\\
b&a\end{bmatrix}\mathbf{U}^{-1}\bm{\Lambda}^{t}\mathbf{U}\begin{bmatrix}1\\
1\end{bmatrix}

\displaystyle=\begin{bmatrix}-\left(\left(1-r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{+}}\right)^{2}r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{+}}^{t}-\left(1-r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{-}}\right)^{2}r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{-}}^{t}\right)\\
-\left(\left(1-r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{+}}\right)^{2}r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{+}}^{t}-\left(1-r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{-}}\right)^{2}r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{-}}^{t}\right)\end{bmatrix}

\displaystyle=-\left(\left(1-r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{+}}\right)^{2}r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{+}}^{t}-\left(1-r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{-}}\right)^{2}r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{-}}^{t}\right)\begin{bmatrix}1\\
1\end{bmatrix}.

\displaystyle{\scriptstyle\bm{\Delta}}\begin{bmatrix}b&a\\
b&a\end{bmatrix}\mathbf{U}^{-1}\bm{\Lambda}^{t}\mathbf{U}\begin{bmatrix}1\\
1\end{bmatrix}\otimes{\scriptstyle\bm{\Delta}}\begin{bmatrix}b&a\\
b&a\end{bmatrix}\mathbf{U}^{-1}\bm{\Lambda}^{t}\mathbf{U}\begin{bmatrix}1\\
1\end{bmatrix}

\displaystyle=\left(\left(1-r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{+}}\right)^{2}r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{+}}^{t}-\left(1-r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{-}}\right)^{2}r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{-}}^{t}\right)^{2}\begin{bmatrix}1&1\\
1&1\end{bmatrix},

\displaystyle{\scriptstyle\bm{\Delta}}^{2}\begin{bmatrix}b&a\\
b&a\end{bmatrix}\left(\Gamma^{t}\right)^{\top}\cdot\aleph\cdot\Gamma^{t}\begin{bmatrix}b&a\\
b&a\end{bmatrix}^{\top}

\displaystyle=\left(\left(1-r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{+}}\right)^{2}r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{+}}^{t}-\left(1-r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{-}}\right)^{2}r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{-}}^{t}\right)^{2}\begin{bmatrix}1&1\\
1&1\end{bmatrix}.

\displaystyle\begin{bmatrix}b&a\\
b&a\end{bmatrix}\sum_{t=0}^{\infty}\left(\Gamma^{t}\right)^{\top}\cdot\aleph\cdot\Gamma^{t}\begin{bmatrix}b&a\\
b&a\end{bmatrix}^{\top}

\displaystyle=\left[\sum_{t=0}^{\infty}\left(\frac{\left(1-r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{+}}\right)^{2}r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{+}}^{t}-\left(1-r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{-}}\right)^{2}r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{-}}^{t}}{{\scriptstyle\bm{\Delta}}}\right)^{2}\right]\begin{bmatrix}1&1\\
1&1\end{bmatrix}.

Using Lemma
39
,

\displaystyle\begin{bmatrix}b&a\\
b&a\end{bmatrix}\sum_{t=0}^{\infty}\left(\Gamma^{t}\right)^{\top}\cdot\aleph\cdot\Gamma^{t}\begin{bmatrix}b&a\\
b&a\end{bmatrix}^{\top}=\left(\frac{2a}{b(4-(a+2b))}+\frac{a+2b}{(4-(a+2b))}\right)\begin{bmatrix}1&1\\
1&1\end{bmatrix}.

Lemma 39

With
r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{+}},r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{-}}
defined by Property
2
, and
\nu(t)
defined by

\displaystyle\nu(t)\overset{\scriptstyle\mathrm{def}}{=}\left[\frac{\left(1-r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{+}}\right)^{2}r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{+}}^{t}-\left(1-r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{-}}\right)^{2}r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{-}}^{t}}{{\scriptstyle\bm{\Delta}}}\right]^{2}.

the series

\displaystyle\sum_{t=0}^{\infty}\nu(t)=\frac{2a}{b(4-(a+2b))}+\frac{a+2b}{(4-(a+2b))}.

Proof

\displaystyle{\scriptstyle\bm{\Delta}}^{2}~\left(\sum_{t=0}^{\infty}\nu(t)\right)

\displaystyle=\sum_{t=0}^{\infty}\left[\left(1-r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{+}}\right)^{4}r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{+}}^{2t}+\left(1-r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{-}}\right)^{4}r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{-}}^{2t}-2\left(1-r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{+}}\right)^{2}\left(1-r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{-}}\right)^{2}r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{+}}^{t}r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{-}}^{t}\right].

From Property
2
when
0<a,b<1
then
|r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{+}}|,|r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{-}}|<1
. Hence, the following holds,

\displaystyle\sum_{t=0}^{\infty}r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{+}}^{2t}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{1-r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{+}}^{2}},\quad\sum_{t=0}^{\infty}r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{-}}^{2t}=\frac{1}{1-r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{-}}^{2}},\quad\sum_{t=0}^{\infty}r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{+}}^{t}r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{-}}^{t}=\frac{1}{1-r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{+}}r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{-}}}.

\displaystyle{\scriptstyle\bm{\Delta}}^{2}~\left(\sum_{t=0}^{\infty}\nu(t)\right)

\displaystyle=\left(1-r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{+}}\right)^{4}\frac{1}{1-r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{+}}^{2}}+\left(1-r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{-}}\right)^{4}\frac{1}{1-r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{-}}^{2}}-2\left(1-r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{+}}\right)^{2}\left(1-r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{-}}\right)^{2}\frac{1}{1-r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{+}}r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{-}}},

\displaystyle=\left[\frac{\left(1-r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{+}}\right)^{3}}{1+r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{+}}}+\frac{\left(1-r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{-}}\right)^{3}}{1+r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{-}}}-2\frac{\left(1-r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{+}}\right)^{2}\left(1-r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{-}}\right)^{2}}{1-r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{+}}r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{-}}}\right].

\displaystyle{\scriptstyle\bm{\Delta}}^{2}~\left(\sum_{t=0}^{\infty}\nu(t)\right)=\left[\frac{\left(1-r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{+}}\right)^{3}}{1+r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{+}}}+\frac{\left(1-r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{-}}\right)^{3}}{1+r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{-}}}-2\frac{\left(1-r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{+}}\right)^{2}\left(1-r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{-}}\right)^{2}}{1-r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{+}}r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{-}}}\right].

(87)

\displaystyle\begin{aligned} {\scriptstyle\bm{\Delta}}^{2}~\left(\sum_{t=0}^{\infty}\nu(t)\right)&=\frac{\left[\left(1-r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{+}}\right)^{3}\left(1+r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{-}}\right)+\left(1-r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{-}}\right)^{3}\left(1+r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{+}}\right)\right]\left[1-r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{+}}r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{-}}\right]}{\left(1+r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{+}}\right)\left(1+r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{-}}\right)\left[1-r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{+}}r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{-}}\right]}\\
&\hskip 56.9055pt-\frac{2\left(1-r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{+}}\right)^{2}\left(1-r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{-}}\right)^{2}\left(1+r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{-}}\right)\left(1+r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{+}}\right)}{\left(1+r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{+}}\right)\left(1+r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{-}}\right)\left[1-r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{+}}r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{-}}\right]}.\end{aligned}

(88)

Note,

\left(1+r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{-}}\right)\left(1+r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{+}}\right)=1+r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{-}}+r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{+}}+r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{-}}r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{+}}.

Using
r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{-}}+r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{+}}=2-(a+b)
,
r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{-}}r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{+}}=1-b
,

\displaystyle\left(1+r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{-}}\right)\left(1+r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{+}}\right)=4-(a+2b).

(89)

Using

\displaystyle 2\left[1-r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{+}}r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{-}}\right]

\displaystyle=\left(1-r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{+}}\right)\left(1+r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{-}}\right)+\left(1+r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{+}}\right)\left(1-r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{-}}\right),

\displaystyle 2\left[1-r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{+}}r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{-}}\right]\left(1-r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{+}}\right)^{3}\left(1+r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{-}}\right)

\displaystyle=\left[\left(1-r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{+}}\right)\left(1+r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{-}}\right)+\left(1+r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{+}}\right)\left(1-r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{-}}\right)\right]\left(1-r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{+}}\right)^{3}\left(1+r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{-}}\right).

\displaystyle 2\left[1-r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{+}}r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{-}}\right]\left(1-r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{+}}\right)^{3}\left(1+r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{-}}\right)

\displaystyle=\left(1-r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{+}}\right)^{4}\left(1+r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{-}}\right)^{2}+\left(1-r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{-}}^{2}\right)\left(1-r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{+}}^{2}\right)\left(1-r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{+}}\right)^{2},

(90)

Symetrically,

\displaystyle 2\left[1-r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{+}}r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{-}}\right]\left(1-r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{-}}\right)^{3}\left(1+r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{+}}\right)

\displaystyle=\left(1-r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{-}}\right)^{4}\left(1+r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{+}}\right)^{2}+\left(1-r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{+}}^{2}\right)\left(1-r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{-}}^{2}\right)\left(1-r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{-}}\right)^{2}.

(91)

\displaystyle\begin{aligned} 4\left(1-r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{+}}\right)^{2}\left(1-r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{-}}\right)^{2}\left(1+r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{-}}\right)\left(1+r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{+}}\right)&=2\left(1+r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{-}}\right)\left(1-r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{+}}\right)^{2}\left(1+r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{+}}\right)\left(1-r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{-}}\right)^{2}\\
&\hskip 28.45274pt+2\left(1-r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{-}}^{2}\right)\left(1-r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{+}}^{2}\right)\left(1-r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{-}}\right)\left(1-r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{+}}\right).\end{aligned}

(92)

Combining the above calculations,

\displaystyle\textrm{Eq}.(\ref{lem:series:p1})+\textrm{Eq}.(\ref{lem:series:p2})-\textrm{Eq}.(\ref{lem:series:p3})

\displaystyle=\left[\left(1-r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{+}}\right)^{2}\left(1+r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{-}}\right)-\left(1-r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{-}}\right)^{2}\left(1+r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{+}}\right)\right]^{2}+\left(1-r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{-}}^{2}\right)\left(1-r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{+}}^{2}\right)\left[(1-r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{+}})-(1-r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{-}})\right]^{2},

(93)

Computing the two terms,

\displaystyle\left(1-r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{+}}\right)^{2}\left(1+r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{-}}\right)-\left(1-r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{-}}\right)^{2}\left(1+r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{+}}\right)

\displaystyle=\left(1-r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{+}}\right)^{2}\left(2-(1-r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{-}})\right)-\left(1-r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{-}}\right)^{2}\left(2-(1-r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{+}})\right),

\displaystyle=2\left[\left(1-r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{+}}\right)^{2}-\left(1-r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{-}}\right)^{2}\right]-\left[\left(1-r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{+}}\right)^{2}\left(1-r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{-}}\right)-\left(1-r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{-}}\right)^{2}\left(1-r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{+}}\right)\right],

\displaystyle=2\left(r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{-}}-r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{+}}\right)\left(2-r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{-}}-r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{+}}\right)-\left(1-r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{-}}\right)\left(1-r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{+}}\right)\left(r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{-}}-r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{+}}\right),

\displaystyle=\left(r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{-}}-r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{+}}\right)\left(4-2r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{-}}-2r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{+}}-1+r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{-}}+r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{+}}-r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{-}}r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{+}}\right)

\displaystyle=-{\scriptstyle\bm{\Delta}}\left(4-\left(1+r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{-}}\right)\left(1+r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{+}}\right)\right)=-{\scriptstyle\bm{\Delta}}(a+2b),\quad\textrm{ from {Eq}.(\ref{lem:series:p5}) },

\displaystyle\left(1-r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{-}}^{2}\right)\left(1-r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{+}}^{2}\right)\left[(1-r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{+}})-(1-r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{-}})\right]^{2}

\displaystyle={\scriptstyle\bm{\Delta}}^{2}\left(1-r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{-}}\right)\left(1-r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{+}}\right)\left(1+r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{-}}\right)\left(1+r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{+}}\right),

\displaystyle={\scriptstyle\bm{\Delta}}^{2}a(4-(a+2b)).

The numerator of Eq.(
88
) as per Eq.(
93
) is
\frac{1}{2}\left({\scriptstyle\bm{\Delta}}^{2}a(4-(a+2b))+{\scriptstyle\bm{\Delta}}^{2}(a+2b)^{2}\right)
. From Eq.(
89
) , the denominator is

\displaystyle\left(1+r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{-}}\right)\left(1+r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{+}}\right)\left(1-r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{+}}r_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{-}}\right)

\displaystyle=b(4-(a+2b)).

(94)

Now from Eq.(
88
), we have

\displaystyle{\scriptstyle\bm{\Delta}}^{2}~\left(\sum_{t=0}^{\infty}\nu(t)\right)

\displaystyle={\scriptstyle\bm{\Delta}}^{2}\left[\frac{a(4-(a+2b))+(a+2b)^{2}}{2b(4-(a+2b))}\right].

Hence,

\displaystyle\left(\sum_{t=0}^{\infty}\nu(t)\right)

\displaystyle=\left[\frac{a(4-(a+2b))+(a+2b)^{2}}{2b(4-(a+2b))}\right],

\displaystyle=\frac{4a+(a+2b)(a+2b-a)}{2b(4-(a+2b))}=\frac{4a}{2b(4-(a+2b))}+\frac{2b(a+2b)}{2b(4-(a+2b))},

\displaystyle=\frac{2a}{b(4-(a+2b))}+\frac{a+2b}{(4-(a+2b))}.

This completes the proof.
